import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';

class EnvConfig {
  static String get baseUrl {
    if (kDebugMode) {
      // Use 10.0.2.2 for Android Emulator, localhost for Web/Desktop
      return 'http://localhost:5000/api';
    }
    return 'https://your-production-domain.com/api';
  }

  static bool get isDevelopment => kDebugMode;
  static bool get isProduction => !kDebugMode;

  static const int connectTimeout = 30000;
  static const int receiveTimeout = 30000;
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class AppColors {
  // Dark Theme Colors
  static const Color backgroundDark = Color(0xFF0F172A);
  static const Color surfaceDark = Color(0xFF1E293B);
  static const Color primaryAccent = Color(0xFF2563EB);
  static const Color secondaryAccent = Color(0xFF7C3AED);
  static const Color successColor = Color(0xFF10B981);
  static const Color textPrimaryDark = Color(0xFFF8FAFC);
  static const Color textSecondaryDark = Color(0xFF94A3B8);

  // Light Theme Colors
  static const Color backgroundLight = Color(0xFFF8FAFC);
  static const Color surfaceLight = Color(0xFFFFFFFF);
  static const Color textPrimaryLight = Color(0xFF0F172A);
  static const Color textSecondaryLight = Color(0xFF64748B);

  // Role Card Colors
  static const Color roleCardDark = Color(0xFF1E293B);
  static const Color roleCardLight = Color(0xFFF1F5F9);
  static const Color roleCardBorderDark = Color(0xFF334155);
  static const Color roleCardBorderLight = Color(0xFFE2E8F0);

  // Gradients
  static const LinearGradient primaryGradient = LinearGradient(
    colors: [primaryAccent, secondaryAccent],
    begin: Alignment.topLeft,
    end: Alignment.bottomRight,
  );

  static const LinearGradient darkGradient = LinearGradient(
    colors: [Color(0xFF3B82F6), Color(0xFF5B21B6)],
    begin: Alignment.topLeft,
    end: Alignment.bottomRight,
  );

  static const LinearGradient cardGradient = LinearGradient(
    colors: [Color(0xFF1E293B), Color(0xFF0F172A)],
    begin: Alignment.topLeft,
    end: Alignment.bottomRight,
  );

  // Helper methods for dynamic colors
  static Color getBackgroundColor(bool isDark) =>
      isDark ? backgroundDark : backgroundLight;

  static Color getSurfaceColor(bool isDark) =>
      isDark ? surfaceDark : surfaceLight;

  static Color getTextPrimaryColor(bool isDark) =>
      isDark ? textPrimaryDark : textPrimaryLight;

  static Color getTextSecondaryColor(bool isDark) =>
      isDark ? textSecondaryDark : textSecondaryLight;

  static Color getRoleCardColor(bool isDark) =>
      isDark ? roleCardDark : roleCardLight;

  static Color getRoleCardBorderColor(bool isDark) =>
      isDark ? roleCardBorderDark : roleCardBorderLight;

  static LinearGradient getPrimaryGradient(bool isDark) =>
      isDark ? darkGradient : primaryGradient;

  static LinearGradient getPrimaryGradientFromContext(BuildContext context) {
    final isDark = Theme.of(context).brightness == Brightness.dark;
    return isDark ? darkGradient : primaryGradient;
  }

  static Color getRoleCardColorFromContext(BuildContext context) {
    final isDark = Theme.of(context).brightness == Brightness.dark;
    return isDark ? roleCardDark : roleCardLight;
  }

  static Color getRoleCardBorderColorFromContext(BuildContext context) {
    final isDark = Theme.of(context).brightness == Brightness.dark;
    return isDark ? roleCardBorderDark : roleCardBorderLight;
  }

  static Color getTextSecondaryColorFromContext(BuildContext context) {
    final isDark = Theme.of(context).brightness == Brightness.dark;
    return isDark ? textSecondaryDark : textSecondaryLight;
  }

  static Color getTextPrimaryColorFromContext(BuildContext context) {
    final isDark = Theme.of(context).brightness == Brightness.dark;
    return isDark ? textPrimaryDark : textPrimaryLight;
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:google_fonts/google_fonts.dart';

class AppTextStyles {
  static TextStyle headingLarge(BuildContext context) => GoogleFonts.cairo(
    fontSize: 28,
    fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
    color: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyLarge?.color,
  );

  static TextStyle headingMedium(BuildContext context) => GoogleFonts.cairo(
    fontSize: 22,
    fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
    color: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyLarge?.color,
  );

  static TextStyle headingSmall(BuildContext context) => GoogleFonts.cairo(
    fontSize: 18,
    fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
    color: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyLarge?.color,
  );

  static TextStyle bodyLarge(BuildContext context) => GoogleFonts.cairo(
    fontSize: 16,
    fontWeight: FontWeight.w400,
    color: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyLarge?.color,
  );

  static TextStyle bodyMedium(BuildContext context) => GoogleFonts.cairo(
    fontSize: 14,
    fontWeight: FontWeight.w400,
    color: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium?.color,
  );

  static TextStyle bodySmall(BuildContext context) => GoogleFonts.cairo(
    fontSize: 12,
    fontWeight: FontWeight.w400,
    color: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall?.color,
  );

  static TextStyle highlight(BuildContext context) => GoogleFonts.cairo(
    fontSize: 14,
    fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
    color: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyLarge?.color,
  );

  static TextStyle buttonText(BuildContext context) => GoogleFonts.cairo(
    fontSize: 16,
    fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
    color: Colors.white,
  );
}

FILE: ..\edutech_app\lib\core\constants\user_role.dart

enum UserRole { student, teacher, parent, superAdmin, supervisor, assistant, pendingTeacher }

extension UserRoleExtension on UserRole {
  String get displayName {
    switch (this) {
      case UserRole.student:
        return 'Student';
      case UserRole.teacher:
        return 'Teacher';
      case UserRole.parent:
        return 'Parent';
      case UserRole.superAdmin:
        return 'Super Admin';
      case UserRole.supervisor:
        return 'Supervisor';
      case UserRole.assistant:
        return 'Assistant';
      case UserRole.pendingTeacher:
        return 'Pending Teacher';
    }
  }

  String get route {
    switch (this) {
      case UserRole.student:
        return '/student';
      case UserRole.teacher:
      case UserRole.superAdmin:
      case UserRole.supervisor:
      case UserRole.assistant:
      case UserRole.pendingTeacher:
        return '/teacher/dashboard';
      case UserRole.parent:
        return '/parent';
    }
  }
}

FILE: ..\edutech_app\lib\core\providers\auth_provider.dart

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';
import '../constants/user_role.dart';
import '../../models/user_model.dart';
import '../services/auth_service.dart';

class AuthProvider extends ChangeNotifier {
  final AuthService _authService = AuthService();

  UserRole? _currentRole;
  String? _userName;
  String? _userPhoneNumber;
  String? _userId;
  String? _subjectId;
  bool _isAuthenticated = false;
  bool _isInitialized = false;
  bool _isLoading = false;
  bool _isVerified = false;
  List<String> _permissions = [];

  UserRole? get currentRole => _currentRole;
  String? get userName => _userName;
  String? get userPhoneNumber => _userPhoneNumber;
  String? get userId => _userId;
  String? get subjectId => _subjectId;
  bool get isAuthenticated => _isAuthenticated;
  bool get isInitialized => _isInitialized;
  bool get isLoading => _isLoading;
  bool get isVerified => _isVerified;
  List<String> get permissions => _permissions;

  bool get isTeacher => _currentRole == UserRole.teacher;
  bool get isStudent => _currentRole == UserRole.student;
  bool get isParent => _currentRole == UserRole.parent;
  bool get isSuperAdmin => _currentRole == UserRole.superAdmin;
  bool get isSupervisor => _currentRole == UserRole.supervisor;
  bool get isAssistant => _currentRole == UserRole.assistant;
  bool get isPendingTeacher => _currentRole == UserRole.pendingTeacher;
  bool get isTeacherOrHigher =>
      isSuperAdmin || isTeacher || isSupervisor || isAssistant;

  bool hasPermission(String permission) {
    if (isSuperAdmin) return true;
    return _permissions.contains(permission);
  }

  void setPermissions(List<String> permissions) {
    _permissions = permissions;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  bool get canUploadVideo => isSuperAdmin || (isTeacher && _isVerified);
  bool get canCreateQuiz => isSuperAdmin || (isTeacher && _isVerified);
  bool get canViewOnly => isSupervisor;
  bool get canAccessAttendance => isSuperAdmin || isTeacher || isAssistant;

  Future<void> initialize() async {
    if (_isInitialized) return;

    _isLoading = true;
    notifyListeners();

    try {
      final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
      final isLoggedIn = prefs.getBool('isLoggedIn') ?? false;
      final roleString = prefs.getString('userRole');
      final userName = prefs.getString('userName');
      final userPhone = prefs.getString('userPhone');
      final isVerified = prefs.getBool('isVerified') ?? false;
      final subjectId = prefs.getString('subjectId');

      if (isLoggedIn && roleString != null) {
        _currentRole = UserRole.values.firstWhere(
          (r) => r.name == roleString,
          orElse: () => UserRole.student,
        );
        _userName = userName;
        _userPhoneNumber = userPhone;
        _subjectId = subjectId;
        _isVerified = isVerified;
        _isAuthenticated = true;
      }
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('AuthProvider init error: $e');
    } finally {
      _isLoading = false;
      _isInitialized = true;
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  Future<bool> login({required String phone, required String password}) async {
    _isLoading = true;
    notifyListeners();

    try {
      final response = await _authService.login(
        phone: phone,
        password: password,
      );

      _userId = response.user.id;
      _userName = response.user.name;
      _userPhoneNumber = response.user.phone;
      _subjectId = response.user.subjectId;
      _isVerified = response.user.isVerified;
      _isAuthenticated = true;

      _updateRoleFromString(response.user.role);

      _isLoading = false;
      notifyListeners();
      return true;
    } catch (e) {
      _isLoading = false;
      notifyListeners();
      rethrow;
    }
  }

  void _updateRoleFromString(String role) {
    switch (role.toUpperCase()) {
      case 'SUPER_ADMIN':
        _currentRole = UserRole.superAdmin;
        break;
      case 'TEACHER':
        _currentRole = UserRole.teacher;
        break;
      case 'STUDENT':
        _currentRole = UserRole.student;
        break;
      case 'PARENT':
        _currentRole = UserRole.parent;
        break;
      default:
        _currentRole = UserRole.student;
    }
  }

  Future<void> logout() async {
    _isLoading = true;
    notifyListeners();

    try {
      await _authService.logout();
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Logout error: $e');
    } finally {
      _resetState();
    }
  }

  void _resetState() {
    _currentRole = null;
    _userName = null;
    _userPhoneNumber = null;
    _userId = null;
    _subjectId = null;
    _isAuthenticated = false;
    _isVerified = false;
    _isLoading = false;
    _permissions = [];
    notifyListeners();
  }

  static Future<void> forceLogout() async {
    await AuthService.clearAllData();
  }

  void updateRole(UserRole role) {
    _currentRole = role;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<void> signup({
    required UserRole role,
    required String name,
    required String phoneNumber,
    required String password,
    String? parentPhoneNumber,
  }) async {
    _isLoading = true;
    notifyListeners();

    try {
      final response = await _authService.register(
        name: name,
        phone: phoneNumber,
        password: password,
        role: role.name.toUpperCase(),
      );

      _currentRole = role;
      _userName = response.user.name;
      _userPhoneNumber = response.user.phone;
      _userId = response.user.id;
      _isAuthenticated = true;
      _isVerified = response.user.isVerified;

      final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
      await prefs.setBool('isLoggedIn', true);
      await prefs.setString('userRole', role.name);
      await prefs.setString('userName', response.user.name);
      await prefs.setString('userPhone', response.user.phone);
      if (role == UserRole.student && parentPhoneNumber != null) {
        await prefs.setString('parentPhoneNumber', parentPhoneNumber);
      }

      _isLoading = false;
      notifyListeners();
    } catch (e) {
      _isLoading = false;
      notifyListeners();
      rethrow;
    }
  }

  Future<void> loginWithRole({
    required UserRole role,
    String? name,
    String? phoneNumber,
  }) async {
    _currentRole = role;
    _userName = name ?? 'User';
    _userPhoneNumber = phoneNumber;
    _isAuthenticated = true;
    _isVerified = true;
    notifyListeners();

    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    await prefs.setBool('isLoggedIn', true);
    await prefs.setString('userRole', role.name);
    if (name != null) await prefs.setString('userName', name);
    if (phoneNumber != null) await prefs.setString('userPhone', phoneNumber);
  }

  void updateUserRole(String phoneNumber, String newRole) {
    debugPrint('updateUserRole called for $phoneNumber -> $newRole');
    notifyListeners();
  }

  void updateUserApproval(String phoneNumber, bool isApproved) {
    debugPrint('updateUserApproval called for $phoneNumber -> $isApproved');
    notifyListeners();
  }

  List<UserModel> getLinkedChildren(String parentPhone) {
    return [];
  }

  UserModel? getCurrentUser() {
    if (_userPhoneNumber == null) return null;
    return UserModel(
      id: _userId ?? '',
      name: _userName ?? 'User',
      phoneNumber: _userPhoneNumber!,
      role: _currentRole?.name ?? 'student',
      isApproved: true,
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import '../../models/user_model.dart';

enum UserFilter { all, students, teachers, parents, pendingTeachers }

enum AttendanceStatus { present, absent }

enum HomeworkStatus { submitted, pending }

class LessonVideo {
  final String id;
  final String title;
  final String description;
  final String courseName;
  final String videoUrl;
  final DateTime createdAt;

  LessonVideo({
    required this.id,
    required this.title,
    required this.description,
    required this.courseName,
    required this.videoUrl,
    required this.createdAt,
  });
}

class VideoAttendance {
  final String studentId;
  final String lessonId;
  final DateTime watchedAt;
  final bool isPresent;

  VideoAttendance({
    required this.studentId,
    required this.lessonId,
    required this.watchedAt,
    required this.isPresent,
  });
}

class SubscriptionHistory {
  final String id;
  final String planName;
  final DateTime startDate;
  final DateTime endDate;
  final double amount;

  SubscriptionHistory({
    required this.id,
    required this.planName,
    required this.startDate,
    required this.endDate,
    required this.amount,
  });
}

class StudentAttendance {
  final String studentId;
  final DateTime date;
  final AttendanceStatus status;

  StudentAttendance({
    required this.studentId,
    required this.date,
    required this.status,
  });
}

class StudentHomework {
  final String id;
  final String studentId;
  final String title;
  final String description;
  final DateTime dueDate;
  final HomeworkStatus status;

  StudentHomework({
    required this.id,
    required this.studentId,
    required this.title,
    required this.description,
    required this.dueDate,
    required this.status,
  });
}

class TeacherManagementProvider extends ChangeNotifier {

      createdAt: DateTime.now().subtract(const Duration(days: 1)),
    ),
  ];

  final Map<String, VideoAttendance> _videoAttendanceRecords = {};

  UserFilter get currentFilter => _currentFilter;
  int get selectedSegment => _selectedSegment;

  List<UserModel> filteredUsers(UserModel? currentUser) {
    if (currentUser == null) return [];

    List<UserModel> baseList = [];

    if (currentUser.role == 'superAdmin') {
      baseList = _allUsers;
    } else if (currentUser.role == 'teacher') {
      final teacherSubject = currentUser.subjectId;
      if (teacherSubject == null) return [];

      // Teachers only see students enrolled in their subject
      final enrolledStudents = _allUsers.where((u) =>
          u.role == 'student' &&
          u.enrolledSubjectIds != null &&
          u.enrolledSubjectIds!.contains(teacherSubject));

      // And the parents of those students
      final parentPhones =
          enrolledStudents.map((s) => s.parentPhoneNumber).toSet();
      final linkedParents = _allUsers.where(
          (u) => u.role == 'parent' && parentPhones.contains(u.phoneNumber));

      baseList = [...enrolledStudents, ...linkedParents];
    } else {
      // Logic for others (supervisor, assistant) - assuming they see students/teachers
      baseList = _allUsers.where((u) => u.role != 'superAdmin').toList();
    }

    switch (_currentFilter) {
      case UserFilter.all:
        return baseList;
      case UserFilter.students:
        return baseList.where((u) => u.role == 'student').toList();
      case UserFilter.teachers:
        return baseList.where((u) => u.role == 'teacher').toList();
      case UserFilter.parents:
        return baseList.where((u) => u.role == 'parent').toList();
      case UserFilter.pendingTeachers:
        return baseList.where((u) => u.role == 'pendingTeacher').toList();
    }
  }

  List<UserModel> get students =>
      _allUsers.where((u) => u.role == 'student').toList();

  void setFilter(UserFilter filter) {
    _currentFilter = filter;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  void setSelectedSegment(int segment) {
    _selectedSegment = segment;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  List<UserModel> getUsersByRole(String role) {
    return _allUsers.where((u) => u.role == role).toList();
  }

  UserModel? getUserById(String id) {
    try {
      return _allUsers.firstWhere((u) => u.id == id);
    } catch (_) {
      return null;
    }
  }

  // --- Admin Tools ---
  void updateUserRole(String userId, String newRole) {
    final index = _allUsers.indexWhere((u) => u.id == userId);
    if (index != -1) {
      _allUsers[index] = _allUsers[index].copyWith(role: newRole);
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  void toggleTeacherApproval(String userId, bool isApproved) {
    final index = _allUsers.indexWhere((u) => u.id == userId);
    if (index != -1) {
      final String newRole = isApproved ? 'teacher' : 'pendingTeacher';
      _allUsers[index] =
          _allUsers[index].copyWith(isApproved: isApproved, role: newRole);
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  List<StudentHomework> getHomeworkForStudent(String studentId) {
    return _homeworkRecords.where((hw) => hw.studentId == studentId).toList();
  }

  void updateHomeworkStatus(String homeworkId, HomeworkStatus status) {
    final index = _homeworkRecords.indexWhere((hw) => hw.id == homeworkId);
    if (index != -1) {
      _homeworkRecords[index] = StudentHomework(
        id: _homeworkRecords[index].id,
        studentId: _homeworkRecords[index].studentId,
        title: _homeworkRecords[index].title,
        description: _homeworkRecords[index].description,
        dueDate: _homeworkRecords[index].dueDate,
        status: status,
      );
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  void toggleAttendance(
    String studentId,
    DateTime date,
    AttendanceStatus status,
  ) {
    final key = '${studentId}_${date.toIso8601String().split('T')[0]}';
    _attendanceRecords[key] = StudentAttendance(
      studentId: studentId,
      date: date,
      status: status,
    );
    notifyListeners();
  }

  AttendanceStatus? getAttendanceStatus(String studentId, DateTime date) {
    final key = '${studentId}_${date.toIso8601String().split('T')[0]}';
    return _attendanceRecords[key]?.status;
  }

  List<SubscriptionHistory> getSubscriptionHistory(String studentId) {
    return _subscriptionHistories[studentId] ?? [];
  }

  List<Map<String, dynamic>> getAttendanceWithStudents() {
    final today = DateTime.now();
    return students.map((student) {
      final status = getAttendanceStatus(student.id, today);
      return {'student': student, 'status': status ?? AttendanceStatus.present};
    }).toList();
  }

  List<Map<String, dynamic>> getHomeworkWithStudents() {
    return _homeworkRecords.map((hw) {
      final student = getUserById(hw.studentId);
      return {'homework': hw, 'student': student};
    }).toList();
  }

  double get classAttendancePercentage {
    if (students.isEmpty) return 0.0;
    int presentCount = 0;
    final today = DateTime.now();
    for (final student in students) {
      final status = getAttendanceStatus(student.id, today);
      if (status == AttendanceStatus.present) {
        presentCount++;
      }
    }
    return (presentCount / students.length) * 100;
  }

  double get assignmentSubmissionRate {
    if (_homeworkRecords.isEmpty) return 0.0;
    final submittedCount = _homeworkRecords
        .where((hw) => hw.status == HomeworkStatus.submitted)
        .length;
    return (submittedCount / _homeworkRecords.length) * 100;
  }

  double get overallGradeAverage {
    return 85.5;
  }

  List<Map<String, dynamic>> getClassOverview() {
    final today = DateTime.now();
    return students.map((student) {
      final attendanceStatus =
          getAttendanceStatus(student.id, today) ?? AttendanceStatus.present;
      final homework = getHomeworkForStudent(student.id);
      final hasPendingHomework = homework.any(
        (hw) => hw.status == HomeworkStatus.pending,
      );
      return {
        'student': student,
        'isPresent': attendanceStatus == AttendanceStatus.present,
        'hasPendingHomework': hasPendingHomework,
      };
    }).toList();
  }

  List<LessonVideo> get lessonVideos => _lessonVideos;

  LessonVideo? getLessonById(String lessonId) {
    try {
      return _lessonVideos.firstWhere((l) => l.id == lessonId);
    } catch (_) {
      return null;
    }
  }

  void markAsPresent(String studentId, String lessonId) {
    final key = '${studentId}_$lessonId';
    _videoAttendanceRecords[key] = VideoAttendance(
      studentId: studentId,
      lessonId: lessonId,
      watchedAt: DateTime.now(),
      isPresent: true,
    );

    toggleAttendance(studentId, DateTime.now(), AttendanceStatus.present);

    notifyListeners();
  }

  bool hasAttendedLesson(String studentId, String lessonId) {
    final key = '${studentId}_$lessonId';
    return _videoAttendanceRecords[key]?.isPresent ?? false;
  }

  List<Map<String, dynamic>> getVideoAttendanceWithStudents(String lessonId) {
    return students.map((student) {
      final attended = hasAttendedLesson(student.id, lessonId);
      return {'student': student, 'attended': attended};
    }).toList();
  }

  void markHomeworkSubmitted(String studentId, String homeworkId) {
    updateHomeworkStatus(homeworkId, HomeworkStatus.submitted);
    notifyListeners();
  }

  StudentHomework? getHomeworkById(String homeworkId) {
    try {
      return _homeworkRecords.firstWhere((hw) => hw.id == homeworkId);
    } catch (_) {
      return null;
    }
  }

  void overrideAttendance(
    String studentId,
    DateTime date,
    AttendanceStatus status,
  ) {
    toggleAttendance(studentId, date, status);
    notifyListeners();
  }

  void overrideHomeworkStatus(String homeworkId, HomeworkStatus status) {
    updateHomeworkStatus(homeworkId, status);
    notifyListeners();
  }

  String _searchQuery = '';

  String get searchQuery => _searchQuery;

  void setSearchQuery(String query) {
    _searchQuery = query;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  List<UserModel> getSearchResults(UserModel? currentUser) {
    // Determine the base pool generated by the relational visibility rules
    final scopedUsers = filteredUsers(currentUser);

    if (_searchQuery.isEmpty) {
      return scopedUsers;
    }
    
    final query = _searchQuery.toLowerCase();
    return scopedUsers
        .where((user) => user.name.toLowerCase().contains(query))
        .toList();
  }

  List<Map<String, dynamic>> getSearchResultsWithAttendance(
      String lessonId, UserModel? currentUser) {
    final results = getSearchResults(currentUser)
        .where((user) => user.role == 'student')
        .map((student) {
      final attended = hasAttendedLesson(student.id, lessonId);
      return {'student': student, 'attended': attended};
    }).toList();
    return results;
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';

class ThemeProvider extends ChangeNotifier {
  static const String _themeKey = 'theme_mode';

  ThemeMode _themeMode = ThemeMode.dark;
  bool _isInitialized = false;

  ThemeMode get themeMode => _themeMode;
  bool get isDarkMode => _themeMode == ThemeMode.dark;
  bool get isInitialized => _isInitialized;

  Future<void> initialize() async {
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    final themeModeIndex =
        prefs.getInt(_themeKey) ?? 2; // default to dark (index 2)
    _themeMode = ThemeMode.values[themeModeIndex];
    _isInitialized = true;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<void> toggleTheme() async {
    _themeMode = _themeMode == ThemeMode.dark
        ? ThemeMode.light
        : ThemeMode.dark;

    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    await prefs.setInt(_themeKey, _themeMode.index);

    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<void> setThemeMode(ThemeMode mode) async {
    _themeMode = mode;

    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    await prefs.setInt(_themeKey, mode.index);

    notifyListeners();
  }
}

CustomTransitionPage<T> fadeSlideTransition<T>({
  required BuildContext context,
  required GoRouterState state,
  required Widget child,
}) {
  return CustomTransitionPage<T>(
    key: state.pageKey,
    child: child,
    transitionDuration: const Duration(milliseconds: 400),
    reverseTransitionDuration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
    transitionsBuilder: (context, animation, secondaryAnimation, child) {
      final curvedAnimation = CurvedAnimation(
        parent: animation,
        curve: Curves.easeOutCubic,
        reverseCurve: Curves.easeInCubic,
      );

      return FadeTransition(
        opacity: curvedAnimation,
        child: SlideTransition(
          position: Tween<Offset>(
            begin: const Offset(0.1, 0),
            end: Offset.zero,
          ).animate(curvedAnimation),
          child: child,
        ),
      );
    },
  );
}

final GoRouter appRouter = GoRouter(
  initialLocation: '/',
  debugLogDiagnostics: false,
  redirect: (context, state) {
    final authProvider = context.read<AuthProvider>();

    if (!authProvider.isInitialized) {
      return null;
    }

    final isLoggedIn = authProvider.isAuthenticated;
    final currentPath = state.uri.path;

    final publicPaths = ['/', '/login', '/signup'];
    final isPublicPath = publicPaths.contains(currentPath);

    if (!isLoggedIn && !isPublicPath) {
      return '/login';
    }

    if (isLoggedIn && isPublicPath) {
      return authProvider.currentRole?.route ?? '/home';
    }

    if (isLoggedIn && (currentPath == '/login' || currentPath == '/signup')) {
      return authProvider.currentRole?.route ?? '/home';
    }

    if (currentPath == '/teacher/dashboard' &&
        !authProvider.isTeacher &&
        !authProvider.isSuperAdmin &&
        !authProvider.isSupervisor &&
        !authProvider.isAssistant) {
      return authProvider.currentRole?.route ?? '/login';
    }

    if (currentPath == '/student' && !authProvider.isStudent) {
      return authProvider.currentRole?.route ?? '/login';
    }

    if (currentPath == '/parent' && !authProvider.isParent) {
      return authProvider.currentRole?.route ?? '/login';
    }

    if (currentPath == '/admin' && !authProvider.isSuperAdmin) {
      return authProvider.currentRole?.route ?? '/login';
    }

    return null;
  },
  routes: [
    GoRoute(
      path: '/',
      pageBuilder: (context, state) => fadeSlideTransition(
        context: context,
        state: state,
        child: const SplashScreen(),
      ),
    ),
    GoRoute(
      path: '/login',
      pageBuilder: (context, state) => fadeSlideTransition(
        context: context,
        state: state,
        child: const LoginScreen(),
      ),
    ),
    GoRoute(
      path: '/signup',
      pageBuilder: (context, state) => fadeSlideTransition(
        context: context,
        state: state,
        child: const SignupScreen(),
      ),
    ),
    GoRoute(
      path: '/home',
      pageBuilder: (context, state) => fadeSlideTransition(
        context: context,
        state: state,
        child: const HomeScreen(),
      ),
    ),
    GoRoute(
      path: '/admin',
      pageBuilder: (context, state) => fadeSlideTransition(
        context: context,
        state: state,
        child: const AdminDashboardScreen(),
      ),
    ),
    GoRoute(
      path: '/parent',
      pageBuilder: (context, state) => fadeSlideTransition(
        context: context,
        state: state,
        child: const ParentDashboardScreen(),
      ),
    ),
    GoRoute(
      path: '/parent-home',
      pageBuilder: (context, state) => fadeSlideTransition(
        context: context,
        state: state,
        child: const ParentHomeScreen(),
      ),
    ),
    GoRoute(
      path: '/teacher/dashboard',
      pageBuilder: (context, state) => fadeSlideTransition(
        context: context,
        state: state,
        child: const TeacherDashboardScreen(),
      ),
    ),
    GoRoute(
      path: '/teacher/upload',
      pageBuilder: (context, state) => fadeSlideTransition(
        context: context,
        state: state,
        child: const UploadVideoScreen(),
      ),
    ),
    GoRoute(
      path: '/teacher/users',
      pageBuilder: (context, state) => fadeSlideTransition(
        context: context,
        state: state,
        child: const UserDirectoryScreen(),
      ),
    ),
    GoRoute(
      path: '/teacher/attendance_homework',
      pageBuilder: (context, state) => fadeSlideTransition(
        context: context,
        state: state,
        child: const AttendanceHomeworkScreen(),
      ),
    ),
    GoRoute(
      path: '/teacher/student/:id',
      pageBuilder: (context, state) {
        final studentId = state.pathParameters['id'] ?? '';
        return fadeSlideTransition(
          context: context,
          state: state,
          child: Student360Screen(studentId: studentId),
        );
      },
    ),
    GoRoute(
      path: '/student',
      pageBuilder: (context, state) => fadeSlideTransition(
        context: context,
        state: state,
        child: const StudentDashboardScreen(),
      ),
    ),
    GoRoute(

      pageBuilder: (context, state) => fadeSlideTransition(

        child: const ProfileScreen(),
      ),
    ),
    GoRoute(

import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'api_service.dart';
import '../../models/user_model.dart';
import '../../models/api/subject_model.dart';

class AdminUserService {
  static final ApiService _api = ApiService();

  static Future<UserModel> getUserPermissions(String userId) async {
    try {
      final response = await _api.get('/users/$userId/permissions');
      final data = response.data['user'] as Map<String, dynamic>;
      return UserModel.fromJson(data);
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw Exception(
        e.error?.toString() ?? 'Failed to fetch user permissions',
      );
    }
  }

  static Future<UserModel> updateUserPermissions({
    required String userId,
    required List<String> permissions,
    String? subjectId,
  }) async {
    try {
      final response = await _api.patch(
        '/users/$userId/permissions',
        data: {'permissions': permissions, 'subjectId': subjectId},
      );
      final data = response.data['user'] as Map<String, dynamic>;
      return UserModel.fromJson(data);
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw Exception(e.error?.toString() ?? 'Failed to update permissions');
    }
  }

  static Future<List<Subject>> getSubjects() async {
    try {
      final response = await _api.get('/subjects');
      final data = response.data as List<dynamic>;
      return data.map((e) => Subject.fromJson(e)).toList();
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw Exception(e.error?.toString() ?? 'Failed to fetch subjects');
    }
  }
}

FILE: ..\edutech_app\lib\core\services\api_service.dart

import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:flutter_secure_storage/flutter_secure_storage.dart';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:flutter/widgets.dart';
import '../config/env_config.dart';

typedef ErrorCallback = void Function(String message);
typedef UnauthorizedCallback = void Function();
typedef ForbiddenCallback = void Function(String message);

class _SecureLogInterceptor extends Interceptor {
  final bool logRequestBody;
  final bool logResponseBody;

  _SecureLogInterceptor({
    this.logRequestBody = true,
    this.logResponseBody = true,
  });

  @override
  void onRequest(RequestOptions options, RequestInterceptorHandler handler) {
    if (kDebugMode && logRequestBody) {
      debugPrint('📤 REQUEST[${options.method}] => PATH: ${options.path}');

      final maskedData = _maskSensitiveData(options.data);
      if (maskedData != null) {
        debugPrint('   DATA: $maskedData');
      }

      if (options.headers.isNotEmpty) {
        final maskedHeaders = Map<String, dynamic>.from(options.headers);
        maskedHeaders.remove('Authorization');
        maskedHeaders['Authorization'] = '***HIDDEN***';
        debugPrint('   HEADERS: $maskedHeaders');
      }
    }
    handler.next(options);
  }

  @override
  void onResponse(Response response, ResponseInterceptorHandler handler) {
    if (kDebugMode && logResponseBody) {
      debugPrint(
        '📥 RESPONSE[${response.statusCode}] => PATH: ${response.requestOptions.path}',
      );
    }
    handler.next(response);
  }

  @override
  void onError(DioException err, ErrorInterceptorHandler handler) {
    if (kDebugMode) {
      debugPrint(
        '❌ ERROR[${err.response?.statusCode}] => PATH: ${err.requestOptions.path}',
      );

      final maskedData = _maskSensitiveData(err.requestOptions.data);
      debugPrint('   REQUEST DATA: $maskedData');
      debugPrint('   ERROR TYPE: ${err.type}');
      debugPrint('   MESSAGE: ${err.message}');

      if (err.response?.data != null) {
        debugPrint('   RESPONSE DATA: ${err.response?.data}');
      }
    }
    handler.next(err);
  }

  dynamic _maskSensitiveData(dynamic data) {
    if (data == null) return null;

    if (data is Map<String, dynamic>) {
      final masked = _deepCopyAndMask(data);
      return masked;
    }

    if (data is FormData) {
      final maskedMap = <String, dynamic>{};
      for (final entry in data.fields) {
        if (_isSensitiveKey(entry.key)) {
          maskedMap[entry.key] = '***HIDDEN***';
        } else {
          maskedMap[entry.key] = entry.value;
        }
      }
      return maskedMap;
    }

    return data;
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> _deepCopyAndMask(Map<String, dynamic> original) {
    final masked = <String, dynamic>{};
    for (final entry in original.entries) {
      if (_isSensitiveKey(entry.key)) {
        masked[entry.key] = '***HIDDEN***';
      } else if (entry.value is Map<String, dynamic>) {
        masked[entry.key] = _deepCopyAndMask(
          entry.value as Map<String, dynamic>,
        );
      } else if (entry.value is List) {
        masked[entry.key] = entry.value.map((item) {
          if (item is Map<String, dynamic>) {
            return _deepCopyAndMask(item);
          }
          return item;
        }).toList();
      } else {
        masked[entry.key] = entry.value;
      }
    }
    return masked;
  }

  bool _isSensitiveKey(String key) {
    final lowerKey = key.toLowerCase();
    return lowerKey.contains('password') ||
        lowerKey.contains('pass') ||
        lowerKey == 'token' ||
        lowerKey == 'secret' ||
        lowerKey.contains('authorization');
  }
}

class ApiService {
  static String get baseUrl => EnvConfig.baseUrl;

  static const int connectTimeout = 30000;
  static const int receiveTimeout = 30000;

  static final ApiService _instance = ApiService._internal();
  factory ApiService() => _instance;

  late final Dio _dio;
  final FlutterSecureStorage _secureStorage = const FlutterSecureStorage();

  static const String _tokenKey = 'jwt_token';

  static ErrorCallback? onError;
  static UnauthorizedCallback? onUnauthorized;
  static ForbiddenCallback? onForbidden;
  static VoidCallback? onServerError;

  ApiService._internal() {
    _dio = Dio(
      BaseOptions(
        baseUrl: baseUrl,
        connectTimeout: const Duration(milliseconds: connectTimeout),
        receiveTimeout: const Duration(milliseconds: receiveTimeout),
        headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
      ),
    );

    // Add custom secure logging interceptor (only in debug mode)
    _dio.interceptors.addAll([
      _AuthInterceptor(_secureStorage),
      _ErrorInterceptor(),
      _SecureLogInterceptor(
        logRequestBody: kDebugMode,
        logResponseBody: kDebugMode,
      ),
    ]);
  }

  Dio get dio => _dio;

  Future<void> setToken(String token) async {
    await _secureStorage.write(key: _tokenKey, value: token);
  }

  Future<String?> getToken() async {
    return await _secureStorage.read(key: _tokenKey);
  }

  Future<void> clearToken() async {
    await _secureStorage.delete(key: _tokenKey);
  }

  Future<void> clearAll() async {
    await _secureStorage.deleteAll();
  }

  Future<Response<T>> get<T>(
    String path, {
    Map<String, dynamic>? queryParameters,
  }) async {
    try {
      return await _dio.get<T>(path, queryParameters: queryParameters);
    } on DioException {
      rethrow;
    }
  }

  Future<Response<T>> post<T>(
    String path, {
    dynamic data,
    Map<String, dynamic>? queryParameters,
    ProgressCallback? onSendProgress,
  }) async {
    try {
      return await _dio.post<T>(
        path,
        data: data,
        queryParameters: queryParameters,
        onSendProgress: onSendProgress,
      );
    } on DioException {
      rethrow;
    }
  }

  Future<Response<T>> put<T>(String path, {dynamic data}) async {
    try {
      return await _dio.put<T>(path, data: data);
    } on DioException {
      rethrow;
    }
  }

  Future<Response<T>> patch<T>(
    String path, {
    dynamic data,
    Map<String, dynamic>? queryParameters,
  }) async {
    try {
      return await _dio.patch<T>(
        path,
        data: data,
        queryParameters: queryParameters,
      );
    } on DioException {
      rethrow;
    }
  }

  Future<Response<T>> delete<T>(String path) async {
    try {
      return await _dio.delete<T>(path);
    } on DioException {
      rethrow;
    }
  }
}

class _AuthInterceptor extends Interceptor {
  final FlutterSecureStorage _secureStorage;

  _AuthInterceptor(this._secureStorage);

  @override
  void onRequest(
    RequestOptions options,
    RequestInterceptorHandler handler,
  ) async {
    final token = await _secureStorage.read(key: 'jwt_token');

    if (token != null && token.isNotEmpty) {
      options.headers['Authorization'] = 'Bearer $token';
    }

    handler.next(options);
  }
}

class _ErrorInterceptor extends Interceptor {
  @override
  void onError(DioException err, ErrorInterceptorHandler handler) {
    final statusCode = err.response?.statusCode;
    final data = err.response?.data;
    String message;
    String? errorType;

    if (_isCorsError(err)) {
      message =
          'Cannot connect to server. Please check your internet connection or server status.';
      errorType = 'CORS_ERROR';
    } else if (statusCode == 401 && _isTokenExpired(err)) {
      _handleTokenExpired();
      message = 'Session expired. Please login again.';
      errorType = 'TOKEN_EXPIRED';
    } else if (statusCode == 401 && _isWrongCredentials(err)) {
      message = 'Invalid phone number or password. Please try again.';
      errorType = 'WRONG_CREDENTIALS';
    } else if (statusCode == 401) {
      _handleUnauthorized();
      message = 'Unauthorized. Please login again.';
      errorType = 'UNAUTHORIZED';
    } else {
      switch (statusCode) {
        case 400:
          message = data?['error'] ?? 'Bad request. Please check your input.';
          break;
        case 401:
          _handleUnauthorized();
          message = 'Session expired. Please login again.';
          break;
        case 403:
          message = _handleForbidden(data);
          break;
        case 404:
          message = 'Resource not found.';
          break;
        case 500:
          _handleServerError();
          message = 'Server under maintenance. Please try again later.';
          break;
        case 502:
        case 503:
          message = 'Service temporarily unavailable.';
          break;
        default:
          message = _getErrorMessage(err);
      }
    }

    if (kDebugMode) {
      print('API Error [$statusCode]: $message');
      print('Error Type: $errorType');
      print('Response: $data');
    }

    final customError = DioException(
      requestOptions: err.requestOptions,
      response: err.response,
      type: err.type,
      error: message,
    );

    (customError as dynamic).errorType = errorType ?? _getErrorType(statusCode);
    (customError as dynamic).statusCode = statusCode;

    handler.next(customError);
  }

  bool _isCorsError(DioException err) {
    if (err.type == DioExceptionType.connectionError ||
        err.type == DioExceptionType.connectionTimeout ||
        err.type == DioExceptionType.receiveTimeout ||
        err.type == DioExceptionType.sendTimeout) {
      return true;
    }

    final message = err.message?.toLowerCase() ?? '';
    if (message.contains('cors') ||
        message.contains('access-control-allow-origin') ||
        message.contains('network error') ||
        message.contains('connection failed')) {
      return true;
    }

    return false;
  }

  bool _isWrongCredentials(DioException err) {
    final data = err.response?.data;
    final errorMessage = data?['error']?.toString().toLowerCase() ?? '';
    return errorMessage.contains('invalid') &&
        (errorMessage.contains('credential') ||
            errorMessage.contains('password'));
  }

  bool _isTokenExpired(DioException err) {
    final data = err.response?.data;
    final errorMessage = data?['error']?.toString().toLowerCase() ?? '';
    return errorMessage.contains('expired') ||
        errorMessage.contains('token') ||
        errorMessage.contains('revoked');
  }

  bool _isUnauthorized(DioException err) {
    final statusCode = err.response?.statusCode;
    return statusCode == 401;
  }

  String _getErrorType(int? statusCode) {
    switch (statusCode) {
      case 400:
        return 'BAD_REQUEST';
      case 401:
        return 'UNAUTHORIZED';
      case 403:
        return 'FORBIDDEN';
      case 404:
        return 'NOT_FOUND';
      case 500:
        return 'SERVER_ERROR';
      default:
        return 'UNKNOWN_ERROR';
    }
  }

  String _getErrorMessage(DioException err) {
    if (err.type == DioExceptionType.connectionTimeout) {
      return 'Connection timeout. Server may be down.';
    } else if (err.type == DioExceptionType.receiveTimeout) {
      return 'Server is taking too long to respond.';
    } else if (err.type == DioExceptionType.connectionError) {
      return 'No internet connection.';
    } else if (err.type == DioExceptionType.cancel) {
      return 'Request cancelled.';
    }
    return 'An unexpected error occurred.';
  }

  void _handleUnauthorized() {
    const storage = FlutterSecureStorage();
    storage.delete(key: 'jwt_token');

    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
      if (ApiService.onUnauthorized != null) {
        ApiService.onUnauthorized!();
      } else {
        navigatorKey.currentState?.pushReplacementNamed('/login');
      }
    });
  }

  void _handleTokenExpired() {
    const storage = FlutterSecureStorage();
    storage.delete(key: 'jwt_token');

    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
      if (ApiService.onUnauthorized != null) {
        ApiService.onUnauthorized!();
      } else {
        navigatorKey.currentState?.pushReplacementNamed('/login');
      }
    });
  }

  String _handleForbidden(dynamic data) {
    final message =
        data?['error'] ?? 'Access denied. You do not have permission.';

    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
      if (ApiService.onForbidden != null) {
        ApiService.onForbidden!(message);
      }
    });

    return message;
  }

  void _handleServerError() {
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
      if (ApiService.onServerError != null) {
        ApiService.onServerError!();
      }
    });
  }
}

final GlobalKey<NavigatorState> navigatorKey = GlobalKey<NavigatorState>();

FILE: ..\edutech_app\lib\core\services\auth_service.dart
--------------------------------------------------

import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:flutter_secure_storage/flutter_secure_storage.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';
import '../services/api_service.dart';
import '../../models/api/user_api_model.dart';

class AuthService {
  final ApiService _apiService = ApiService();
  final FlutterSecureStorage _secureStorage = const FlutterSecureStorage(
    aOptions: AndroidOptions(encryptedSharedPreferences: true),
    iOptions: IOSOptions(accessibility: KeychainAccessibility.first_unlock),
  );

  static const List<String> _secureKeys = ['jwt_token', 'userId'];

  static const List<String> _sharedPrefsKeys = [
    'isLoggedIn',
    'userRole',
    'userName',
    'userPhone',
    'parentPhoneNumber',
    'subjectIds',
    'selectedSubjectId',
    'isVerified',
    'subjectId',
  ];

  Future<AuthResponse> login({
    required String phone,
    required String password,
  }) async {
    try {
      final response = await _apiService.post(
        '/auth/login',
        data: {'phone': phone, 'password': password},
      );

      final authResponse = AuthResponse.fromJson(
        response.data as Map<String, dynamic>,
      );

      // Securely store JWT token using flutter_secure_storage
      await _secureStorage.write(key: 'jwt_token', value: authResponse.token);
      await _secureStorage.write(key: 'userId', value: authResponse.user.id);

      await _saveUserData(authResponse);

      return authResponse;
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw _handleError(e);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _saveUserData(AuthResponse authResponse) async {
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    await prefs.setBool('isLoggedIn', true);
    await prefs.setString('userRole', authResponse.user.role);
    await prefs.setString('userName', authResponse.user.name);
    await prefs.setString('userPhone', authResponse.user.phone);
    await prefs.setBool('isVerified', authResponse.user.isVerified);

    if (authResponse.user.subjectId != null) {
      await prefs.setString('subjectId', authResponse.user.subjectId!);
    }
  }

  Future<AuthResponse> register({
    required String name,
    required String phone,
    required String password,
    String role = 'STUDENT',
  }) async {
    try {
      final response = await _apiService.post(
        '/auth/register',
        data: {
          'name': name,
          'phone': phone,
          'password': password,
          'role': role,
        },
      );

      return AuthResponse.fromJson(response.data as Map<String, dynamic>);
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw _handleError(e);
    }
  }

  Future<void> logout() async {
    try {
      // Call backend logout endpoint to blacklist token
      await _apiService.post('/auth/logout').catchError((_) {});
    } catch (_) {
      // Continue with local logout even if backend fails
    } finally {
      // Always clear local storage
      await clearAllData();
    }
  }

  static Future<void> clearAllData() async {
    // Clear secure storage
    const secureStorage = FlutterSecureStorage(
      aOptions: AndroidOptions(encryptedSharedPreferences: true),
      iOptions: IOSOptions(accessibility: KeychainAccessibility.first_unlock),
    );

    for (final key in _secureKeys) {
      await secureStorage.delete(key: key);
    }

    // Clear shared preferences
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    for (final key in _sharedPrefsKeys) {
      await prefs.remove(key);
    }
  }

  Future<bool> checkAuthStatus() async {
    try {
      // Check for token in secure storage
      final token = await _secureStorage.read(key: 'jwt_token');
      if (token == null || token.isEmpty) {
        return false;
      }

      final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
      return prefs.getBool('isLoggedIn') ?? false;
    } catch (e) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  Future<Map<String, dynamic>?> getStoredUserData() async {
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    return {
      'userRole': prefs.getString('userRole'),
      'userName': prefs.getString('userName'),
      'userPhone': prefs.getString('userPhone'),
      'isVerified': prefs.getBool('isVerified'),
      'subjectId': prefs.getString('subjectId'),
    };
  }

  Future<String?> getStoredToken() async {
    return await _secureStorage.read(key: 'jwt_token');
  }

  String _handleError(DioException e) {
    if (e.response != null) {
      switch (e.response?.statusCode) {
        case 401:
          return 'Invalid phone number or password';
        case 404:
          return 'User not found';
        case 403:
          return e.response?.data?['error'] ??
              'Account not approved. Please contact the administrator.';
        case 400:
          return e.response?.data?['error'] ?? 'Bad request';
        case 409:
          return e.response?.data?['message'] ?? 'Conflict';
        default:
          return e.response?.data?['message'] ?? e.response?.data?['error'] ?? 'An error occurred';
      }
    } else if (e.type == DioExceptionType.connectionTimeout ||
        e.type == DioExceptionType.receiveTimeout) {
      return 'Connection timeout. Please check your internet connection.';
    } else if (e.type == DioExceptionType.connectionError) {
      return 'Unable to connect to server. Please check your internet connection.';
    } else if (e.type == DioExceptionType.cancel) {
      return 'Request cancelled';
    }
    return 'An unexpected error occurred. Please try again later.';
  }
}

import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import '../services/api_service.dart';
import '../../models/api/lesson_model.dart';
import '../../models/api/tracking_model.dart';

class LessonService {
  final ApiService _apiService = ApiService();

  Future<List<Lesson>> getLessons({String? subjectId}) async {
    try {
      final queryParams = subjectId != null ? {'subjectId': subjectId} : null;
      final response = await _apiService.get(
        '/lessons/public',
        queryParameters: queryParams,
      );

      if (response.data is List) {
        return (response.data as List)
            .map((json) => Lesson.fromJson(json as Map<String, dynamic>))
            .toList();
      }
      return [];
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw _handleError(e);
    }
  }

  Future<Lesson> createLesson({
    required String subjectId,
    required String title,
    String? videoUrl,
    String? thumbnailUrl,
    String? homeworkText,
  }) async {
    try {
      final response = await _apiService.post(
        '/lessons/lessons',
        data: {
          'subjectId': subjectId,
          'title': title,
          'videoUrl': videoUrl,
          'thumbnailUrl': thumbnailUrl,
          'homeworkText': homeworkText,
        },
      );

      return Lesson.fromJson(response.data as Map<String, dynamic>);
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw _handleError(e);
    }
  }

  Future<Tracking> markAttendance(String lessonId) async {
    try {
      final response = await _apiService.post(
        '/lessons/mark-attendance',
        data: {'lessonId': lessonId},
      );

      return Tracking.fromJson(response.data as Map<String, dynamic>);
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw _handleError(e);
    }
  }

  Future<QuizResult> submitQuiz({
    required String lessonId,
    required List<int> answers,
  }) async {
    try {
      final response = await _apiService.post(
        '/lessons/submit-quiz',
        data: {'lessonId': lessonId, 'answers': answers},
      );

      return QuizResult.fromJson(response.data as Map<String, dynamic>);
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw _handleError(e);
    }
  }

  Future<List<Map<String, dynamic>>> fetchStudents() async {
    try {
      final response = await _apiService.get('/lessons/fetch-students');
      return (response.data as List<dynamic>).cast<Map<String, dynamic>>();
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw _handleError(e);
    }
  }

  String _handleError(DioException e) {
    if (e.response?.statusCode == 401) {
      return 'Session expired. Please login again.';
    } else if (e.response?.statusCode == 403) {
      return 'You do not have permission to perform this action';
    } else if (e.response?.statusCode == 404) {
      return 'Resource not found';
    } else if (e.response?.statusCode == 400) {
      return e.response?.data?['error'] ?? 'Bad request';
    } else if (e.type == DioExceptionType.connectionTimeout ||
        e.type == DioExceptionType.receiveTimeout) {
      return 'Connection timeout. Please check your internet connection.';
    } else {
      return 'An error occurred. Please try again later.';
    }
  }
}

import 'dart:io';
import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import '../services/api_service.dart';

typedef UploadProgressCallback = void Function(int sent, int total);

class LessonUploadService {
  final ApiService _apiService = ApiService();

  Future<LessonUploadResult> uploadLesson({
    required String title,
    required String description,
    required String subjectId,
    required File videoFile,
    File? thumbnailFile,
    String? homeworkText,
    UploadProgressCallback? onProgress,
  }) async {
    try {
      final formData = FormData.fromMap({
        'title': title,
        'subjectId': subjectId,
        if (description.isNotEmpty) 'description': description,
        'videoFile': await MultipartFile.fromFile(
          videoFile.path,
          filename: videoFile.path.split(Platform.pathSeparator).last,
        ),
        if (thumbnailFile != null)
          'thumbnailFile': await MultipartFile.fromFile(
            thumbnailFile.path,
            filename: thumbnailFile.path.split(Platform.pathSeparator).last,
          ),
        if (homeworkText != null && homeworkText.isNotEmpty)
          'homeworkText': homeworkText,
      });

      final response = await _apiService.post(
        '/lessons/upload',
        data: formData,
        onSendProgress: onProgress,
      );

      return LessonUploadResult.success(
        message: response.data['message'] ?? 'Lesson uploaded successfully',
        lesson: response.data['lesson'],
      );
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      return LessonUploadResult.failure(_handleError(e));
    } catch (e) {
      return LessonUploadResult.failure('Unexpected error: $e');
    }
  }

  String _handleError(DioException e) {
    if (e.response?.statusCode == 400) {
      return e.response?.data?['error'] ?? 'Upload failed';
    } else if (e.response?.statusCode == 401) {
      return 'Session expired. Please login again.';
    } else if (e.response?.statusCode == 403) {
      return e.response?.data?['error'] ?? 'Access denied';
    } else if (e.response?.statusCode == 413) {
      return 'File too large. Maximum size is 500MB.';
    } else if (e.type == DioExceptionType.cancel) {
      return 'Upload cancelled';
    } else if (e.type == DioExceptionType.connectionTimeout) {
      return 'Connection timeout';
    } else if (e.type == DioExceptionType.sendTimeout) {
      return 'Upload too slow. Please check your connection.';
    } else {
      return 'Upload failed. Please try again.';
    }
  }
}

class LessonUploadResult {
  final bool isSuccess;
  final String? message;
  final Map<String, dynamic>? lesson;
  final String? error;

  LessonUploadResult._({
    required this.isSuccess,
    this.message,
    this.lesson,
    this.error,
  });

  factory LessonUploadResult.success({
    required String message,
    Map<String, dynamic>? lesson,
  }) {
    return LessonUploadResult._(
      isSuccess: true,
      message: message,
      lesson: lesson,
    );
  }

  factory LessonUploadResult.failure(String error) {
    return LessonUploadResult._(isSuccess: false, error: error);
  }
}

FILE: ..\edutech_app\lib\core\services\upload_service.dart

import 'dart:io';
import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import '../services/api_service.dart';
import '../../models/api/lesson_model.dart';
import '../../models/api/quiz_model.dart';
import '../../models/api/tracking_model.dart';

typedef ProgressCallback = void Function(int sent, int total);

class UploadService {
  final ApiService _apiService = ApiService();

  Future<UploadResult> uploadLesson({
    required String subjectId,
    required String title,
    required File videoFile,
    required File thumbnailFile,
    File? documentFile,
    String? homeworkText,
    Map<String, dynamic>? quizData,
    ProgressCallback? onProgress,
  }) async {
    try {
      final formData = FormData.fromMap({
        'subjectId': subjectId,
        'title': title,
        'videoFile': await MultipartFile.fromFile(
          videoFile.path,
          filename: videoFile.path.split('/').last,
        ),
        'thumbnailFile': await MultipartFile.fromFile(
          thumbnailFile.path,
          filename: thumbnailFile.path.split('/').last,
        ),
        if (documentFile != null)
          'pdfFile': await MultipartFile.fromFile(
            documentFile.path,
            filename: documentFile.path.split('/').last,
          ),
        'homeworkText': ?homeworkText,
        'quizData': ?quizData,
      });

      final response = await _apiService.post(
        '/lessons/upload',
        data: formData,
        onSendProgress: (int sent, int total) {
          if (onProgress != null) {
            onProgress(sent, total);
          }
        },
      );

      return UploadResult.success(
        message: response.data['message'] ?? 'Upload successful',
        lessonId: response.data['lesson']?['id'],
      );
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      return UploadResult.failure(_handleError(e));
    } catch (e) {
      return UploadResult.failure('Unexpected error: $e');
    }
  }

  Future<LessonWithQuiz> getLessonWithQuiz(String lessonId) async {
    try {
      final response = await _apiService.get('/tracking/$lessonId');
      final data = response.data as Map<String, dynamic>;

      final lesson = Lesson.fromJson(data['lesson'] as Map<String, dynamic>);

      List<Quiz> quizzes = [];
      if (data['quizzes'] != null) {
        quizzes = (data['quizzes'] as List)
            .map((q) => Quiz.fromJson(q as Map<String, dynamic>))
            .toList();
      }

      Tracking? tracking;
      if (data['tracking'] != null) {
        tracking = Tracking.fromJson(data['tracking'] as Map<String, dynamic>);
      }

      return LessonWithQuiz(
        lesson: lesson,
        quizzes: quizzes,
        tracking: tracking,
      );
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw _handleError(e);
    }
  }

  Future<Tracking> markAttendance(String lessonId) async {
    try {
      final response = await _apiService.post(
        '/tracking/attend',
        data: {'lessonId': lessonId},
      );

      return Tracking.fromJson(
        response.data['tracking'] as Map<String, dynamic>,
      );
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw _handleError(e);
    }
  }

  String _handleError(DioException e) {
    if (e.response?.statusCode == 400) {
      return e.response?.data?['error'] ?? 'Upload failed';
    } else if (e.response?.statusCode == 401) {
      return 'Session expired. Please login again.';
    } else if (e.response?.statusCode == 403) {
      return e.response?.data?['error'] ?? 'Access denied';
    } else if (e.response?.statusCode == 413) {
      return 'File too large. Maximum size is 500MB.';
    } else if (e.type == DioExceptionType.cancel) {
      return 'Upload cancelled';
    } else if (e.type == DioExceptionType.connectionTimeout) {
      return 'Connection timeout';
    } else {
      return 'Upload failed. Please try again.';
    }
  }
}

class UploadResult {
  final bool isSuccess;
  final String? message;
  final String? lessonId;
  final String? error;

  UploadResult._({
    required this.isSuccess,
    this.message,
    this.lessonId,
    this.error,
  });

  factory UploadResult.success({required String message, String? lessonId}) {
    return UploadResult._(
      isSuccess: true,
      message: message,
      lessonId: lessonId,
    );
  }

  factory UploadResult.failure(String error) {
    return UploadResult._(isSuccess: false, error: error);
  }
}

class LessonWithQuiz {
  final Lesson lesson;
  final List<Quiz> quizzes;
  final Tracking? tracking;

  LessonWithQuiz({required this.lesson, required this.quizzes, this.tracking});

  Quiz? get activeQuiz => quizzes.isNotEmpty ? quizzes.first : null;
  bool get hasQuiz => quizzes.isNotEmpty;
  bool get isCompleted => tracking?.isPresent == true;
}

FILE: ..\edutech_app\lib\core\services\video_upload_service.dart
--------------------------------------------------

import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:file_picker/file_picker.dart';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import '../services/api_service.dart';

typedef UploadProgressCallback = void Function(int sent, int total);

class VideoUploadService {
  final ApiService _apiService = ApiService();

  static const int maxFileSizeMB = 100;

  Future<FilePickerResult?> pickVideoFile() async {
    return await FilePicker.platform.pickFiles(
      type: FileType.video,
      allowMultiple: false,
    );
  }

  Future<VideoUploadResult> uploadVideo({
    required String title,
    required String subjectId,
    required PlatformFile videoFile,
    PlatformFile? thumbnailFile,
    String? homeworkText,
    UploadProgressCallback? onProgress,
  }) async {
    try {
      if (kDebugMode) {
        debugPrint('=== UPLOAD DEBUG ===');
        debugPrint('File name: ${videoFile.name}');
        debugPrint('File size: ${videoFile.size}');
        debugPrint('Is Web: $kIsWeb');
        debugPrint('Bytes null? ${videoFile.bytes == null}');
      }

      if (videoFile.bytes == null) {
        return VideoUploadResult.failure(
          'No file data available. Please select a file.',
        );
      }

      final formData = FormData();

      formData.fields.add(MapEntry('subjectId', subjectId));
      formData.fields.add(MapEntry('title', title));

      formData.files.add(
        MapEntry(
          'video',
          MultipartFile.fromBytes(
            videoFile.bytes as Uint8List,
            filename: videoFile.name,
          ),
        ),
      );

      if (thumbnailFile != null && thumbnailFile.bytes != null) {
        formData.files.add(
          MapEntry(
            'thumbnail',
            MultipartFile.fromBytes(
              thumbnailFile.bytes as Uint8List,
              filename: thumbnailFile.name,
            ),
          ),
        );
      }

      if (homeworkText != null && homeworkText.isNotEmpty) {
        formData.fields.add(MapEntry('homeworkText', homeworkText));
      }

      if (kDebugMode) {
        debugPrint('FormData created with ${formData.files.length} files');
      }

      final response = await _apiService.dio.post(
        '/lessons/upload',
        data: formData,
        options: Options(headers: {'Content-Type': 'multipart/form-data'}),
        onSendProgress: (sent, total) {
          if (onProgress != null) {
            onProgress(sent, total);
          }
          if (kDebugMode) {
            final percentage = ((sent / total) * 100).toStringAsFixed(0);
            debugPrint('Upload progress: $percentage% ($sent/$total)');
          }
        },
      );

      debugPrint('=== UPLOAD RESPONSE ===');
      debugPrint('Status: ${response.statusCode}');
      debugPrint('Data: ${response.data}');

      if (response.statusCode == 200 || response.statusCode == 201) {
        debugPrint('✅ Upload Success!');
      }

      return VideoUploadResult.success(
        message: response.data['message'] ?? 'Video uploaded successfully',
        lesson: response.data['lesson'],
      );
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      debugPrint('DioException: ${e.type}');
      debugPrint('Response: ${e.response?.data}');
      return VideoUploadResult.failure(_handleError(e));
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Unexpected error: $e');
      return VideoUploadResult.failure('Unexpected error: $e');
    }
  }

  String _handleError(DioException e) {
    if (e.response?.statusCode == 400) {
      return e.response?.data?['error'] ?? 'Upload failed';
    } else if (e.response?.statusCode == 401) {
      return 'Session expired. Please login again.';
    } else if (e.response?.statusCode == 403) {
      return e.response?.data?['error'] ?? 'Access denied';
    } else if (e.response?.statusCode == 413) {
      return 'File too large. Maximum size is $maxFileSizeMB MB.';
    } else if (e.type == DioExceptionType.cancel) {
      return 'Upload cancelled';
    } else if (e.type == DioExceptionType.connectionTimeout) {
      return 'Connection timeout. Please check your internet.';
    } else if (e.type == DioExceptionType.sendTimeout) {
      return 'Upload too slow. Please try a smaller file.';
    }
    return 'Upload failed. Please try again.';
  }
}

class VideoUploadResult {
  final bool isSuccess;
  final String? message;
  final Map<String, dynamic>? lesson;
  final String? error;

  VideoUploadResult._({
    required this.isSuccess,
    this.message,
    this.lesson,
    this.error,
  });

  factory VideoUploadResult.success({
    required String message,
    Map<String, dynamic>? lesson,
  }) {
    return VideoUploadResult._(
      isSuccess: true,
      message: message,
      lesson: lesson,
    );
  }

  factory VideoUploadResult.failure(String error) {
    return VideoUploadResult._(isSuccess: false, error: error);
  }
}

import 'api_service.dart';

class WeeklyProgressService {
  final ApiService _apiService = ApiService();

  Future<WeeklyProgress> getWeeklyProgress() async {
    final response = await _apiService.get('/student/weekly-progress');
    return WeeklyProgress.fromJson(response.data);
  }
}

class WeeklyProgress {
  final double watchedHours;
  final double weeklyGoal;
  final int progressPercentage;
  final double remainingHours;

  WeeklyProgress({
    required this.watchedHours,
    required this.weeklyGoal,
    required this.progressPercentage,
    required this.remainingHours,
  });

  factory WeeklyProgress.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return WeeklyProgress(
      watchedHours: (json['watchedHours'] as num).toDouble(),
      weeklyGoal: (json['weeklyGoal'] as num).toDouble(),
      progressPercentage: json['progressPercentage'] as int,
      remainingHours: (json['remainingHours'] as num).toDouble(),
    );
  }
}

FILE: ..\edutech_app\lib\core\utils\api_result.dart

import 'package:dio/dio.dart';

class AppException implements Exception {
  final String message;
  final int? statusCode;
  final dynamic originalError;

  AppException({required this.message, this.statusCode, this.originalError});

  factory AppException.fromDioException(DioException e) {
    String message;
    int? statusCode = e.response?.statusCode;

    switch (e.type) {
      case DioExceptionType.connectionTimeout:
        message = 'Connection timeout. Please check your internet.';
        break;
      case DioExceptionType.sendTimeout:
        message = 'Send timeout. Please try again.';
        break;
      case DioExceptionType.receiveTimeout:
        message = 'Server is taking too long to respond.';
        break;
      case DioExceptionType.badCertificate:
        message = 'Invalid certificate.';
        break;
      case DioExceptionType.badResponse:
        message = _handleBadResponse(e.response);
        break;
      case DioExceptionType.cancel:
        message = 'Request was cancelled.';
        break;
      case DioExceptionType.connectionError:
        message = 'No internet connection.';
        break;
      case DioExceptionType.unknown:
      default:
        message = e.error?.toString() ?? 'An unexpected error occurred.';
    }

    return AppException(
      message: message,
      statusCode: statusCode,
      originalError: e,
    );
  }

  static String _handleBadResponse(Response? response) {
    if (response == null) return 'No response from server.';

    final data = response.data;
    if (data is Map && data.containsKey('error')) {
      return data['error'].toString();
    }

    switch (response.statusCode) {
      case 400:
        return 'Bad request. Please check your input.';
      case 401:
        return 'Session expired. Please login again.';
      case 403:
        return 'Access denied.';
      case 404:
        return 'Resource not found.';
      case 422:
        return 'Validation error.';
      case 500:
        return 'Server error. Please try again later.';
      case 502:
      case 503:
        return 'Service temporarily unavailable.';
      default:
        return 'An error occurred.';
    }
  }

  @override
  String toString() => message;
}

class ApiResult<T> {
  final T? data;
  final AppException? error;
  final bool isSuccess;

  ApiResult._({this.data, this.error, required this.isSuccess});

  factory ApiResult.success(T data) {
    return ApiResult._(data: data, isSuccess: true);
  }

  factory ApiResult.failure(AppException error) {
    return ApiResult._(error: error, isSuccess: false);
  }

  R when<R>({
    required R Function(T data) success,
    required R Function(AppException error) failure,
  }) {
    if (isSuccess && data != null) {
      return success(data as T);
    } else {
      return failure(error ?? AppException(message: 'Unknown error'));
    }
  }
}

Future<ApiResult<T>> safeApiCall<T>(Future<T> Function() apiCall) async {
  try {
    final data = await apiCall();
    return ApiResult.success(data);
  } on DioException catch (e) {
    return ApiResult.failure(AppException.fromDioException(e));
  } catch (e) {
    return ApiResult.failure(AppException(message: e.toString()));
  }
}
