import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';
import '../../../core/constants/app_colors.dart';
import '../../../core/constants/app_text_styles.dart';
import '../../../core/providers/teacher_management_provider.dart';
import '../../../shared/widgets/glass_card.dart';

class Student360Screen extends StatelessWidget {
  final String studentId;

  const Student360Screen({super.key, required this.studentId});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Directionality(
      textDirection: TextDirection.rtl,
      child: Scaffold(
        backgroundColor: AppColors.backgroundDark,
        appBar: AppBar(
          backgroundColor: AppColors.backgroundDark,
          elevation: 0,
          title: Text(
            'ملف الطالب',
            style: AppTextStyles.headingMedium(context),
          ),
          leading: IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back),
            onPressed: () => context.go('/teacher/users'),
          ),
        ),
        body: Consumer<TeacherManagementProvider>(
          builder: (context, provider, child) {
            final student = provider.getUserById(studentId);

            if (student == null) {
              return Center(
                child: Text(
                  'الطالب غير موجود',
                  style: AppTextStyles.bodyMedium(context),
                ),
              );
            }

            final homework = provider.getHomeworkForStudent(studentId);
            final subscriptionHistory = provider.getSubscriptionHistory(
              studentId,
            );

            return SingleChildScrollView(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  _StudentHeader(
                    name: student.name,
                    phoneNumber: student.phoneNumber,
                    parentPhoneNumber: student.parentPhoneNumber,
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 24),
                  Text('الواجبات', style: AppTextStyles.headingSmall(context)),
                  const SizedBox(height: 12),
                  if (homework.isEmpty)
                    _EmptyState(message: 'لا توجد واجبات')
                  else
                    ...homework.map(
                      (hw) => _HomeworkItem(
                        title: hw.title,
                        description: hw.description,
                        dueDate: hw.dueDate,
                        status: hw.status,
                      ),
                    ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 24),
                  Text(
                    'سجل الاشتراكات',
                    style: AppTextStyles.headingSmall(context),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 12),
                  if (subscriptionHistory.isEmpty)
                    _EmptyState(message: 'لا يوجد سجل اشتراكات')
                  else
                    ...subscriptionHistory.map(
                      (sub) => _SubscriptionItem(
                        planName: sub.planName,
                        startDate: sub.startDate,
                        endDate: sub.endDate,
                        amount: sub.amount,
                      ),
                    ),
                ],
              ),
            );
          },
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _StudentHeader extends StatelessWidget {
  final String name;
  final String phoneNumber;
  final String? parentPhoneNumber;

  const _StudentHeader({
    required this.name,
    required this.phoneNumber,
    this.parentPhoneNumber,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return GlassCard(
      useGradient: true,
      child: Column(
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                  shape: BoxShape.circle,
                ),
                child: const Icon(Icons.person, color: Colors.white, size: 40),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 16),
              Expanded(
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Text(
                      name,
                      style: AppTextStyles.headingSmall(
                        context,
                      ).copyWith(color: Colors.white),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 4),
                    Row(
                      children: [
                        Container(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                            horizontal: 8,
                            vertical: 4,
                          ),
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color: AppColors.primaryAccent.withValues(
                              alpha: 0.2,
                            ),
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                          ),
                          child: Text(
                            'طالب',
                            style: AppTextStyles.bodySmall(
                              context,
                            ).copyWith(color: Colors.white),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),
          const Divider(color: Colors.white24),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          _InfoRow(icon: Icons.phone, label: 'رقم الهاتف', value: phoneNumber),
          if (parentPhoneNumber != null) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            _InfoRow(
              icon: Icons.family_restroom,
              label: 'ولي الأمر',
              value: parentPhoneNumber!,
            ),
          ],
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _InfoRow extends StatelessWidget {
  final IconData icon;
  final String label;
  final String value;

  const _InfoRow({
    required this.icon,
    required this.label,
    required this.value,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Row(
      children: [
        Icon(icon, color: Colors.white70, size: 18),
        const SizedBox(width: 8),
        Text(
          '$label: ',
          style: AppTextStyles.bodySmall(
            context,
          ).copyWith(color: Colors.white70),
        ),
        Text(
          value,
          style: AppTextStyles.bodySmall(
            context,
          ).copyWith(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

class _HomeworkItem extends StatelessWidget {
  final String title;
  final String description;
  final DateTime dueDate;
  final HomeworkStatus status;

  const _HomeworkItem({
    required this.title,
    required this.description,
    required this.dueDate,
    required this.status,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final isSubmitted = status == HomeworkStatus.submitted;
    final daysLeft = dueDate.difference(DateTime.now()).inDays;

    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
      child: GlassCard(
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Row(
              children: [
                Expanded(
                  child: Text(
                    title,
                    style: AppTextStyles.bodyMedium(
                      context,
                    ).copyWith(fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
                  ),
                ),
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                    horizontal: 8,
                    vertical: 4,
                  ),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: isSubmitted
                        ? AppColors.successColor.withValues(alpha: 0.1)
                        : Colors.orange.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                  ),
                  child: Text(
                    isSubmitted ? 'مُسلم' : 'معلق',
                    style: AppTextStyles.bodySmall(context).copyWith(
                      color: isSubmitted
                          ? AppColors.successColor
                          : Colors.orange,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            Text(description, style: AppTextStyles.bodySmall(context)),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            Row(
              children: [
                Icon(
                  Icons.calendar_today,
                  size: 14,
                  color: daysLeft < 0
                      ? Colors.red
                      : AppColors.textSecondaryDark,
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 4),
                Text(
                  daysLeft < 0
                      ? 'متأخر'
                      : daysLeft == 0
                      ? 'اليوم'
                      : 'متبقي $daysLeft يوم',
                  style: AppTextStyles.bodySmall(context).copyWith(
                    color: daysLeft < 0
                        ? Colors.red
                        : AppColors.textSecondaryDark,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _SubscriptionItem extends StatelessWidget {
  final String planName;
  final DateTime startDate;
  final DateTime endDate;
  final double amount;

  const _SubscriptionItem({
    required this.planName,
    required this.startDate,
    required this.endDate,
    required this.amount,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
      child: GlassCard(
        child: Row(
          children: [
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: AppColors.secondaryAccent.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
              ),
              child: const Icon(
                Icons.card_membership,
                color: AppColors.secondaryAccent,
                size: 28,
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 16),
            Expanded(
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Text(
                    planName,
                    style: AppTextStyles.bodyMedium(
                      context,
                    ).copyWith(fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 4),
                  Text(
                    '${startDate.day}/${startDate.month}/${startDate.year} - ${endDate.day}/${endDate.month}/${endDate.year}',
                    style: AppTextStyles.bodySmall(context),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            Text(
              '${amount.toStringAsFixed(0)} ر.س',
              style: AppTextStyles.bodyMedium(context).copyWith(
                color: AppColors.primaryAccent,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            Text(
              message,
              style: AppTextStyles.bodyMedium(
                context,
              ).copyWith(color: AppColors.textSecondaryDark),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';
import '../../../core/constants/app_colors.dart';
import '../../../core/constants/app_text_styles.dart';
import '../../../core/providers/auth_provider.dart';
import '../../../core/providers/teacher_management_provider.dart';

class TeacherDashboardScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const TeacherDashboardScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<TeacherDashboardScreen> createState() => _TeacherDashboardScreenState();
}

class _TeacherDashboardScreenState extends State<TeacherDashboardScreen>
    with AutomaticKeepAliveClientMixin {
  @override
  bool get wantKeepAlive => true;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    super.build(context);
    final authProvider = context.watch<AuthProvider>();
    final teacherProvider = context.watch<TeacherManagementProvider>();

    return Directionality(
      textDirection: TextDirection.rtl,
      child: Scaffold(
        backgroundColor: Theme.of(context).scaffoldBackgroundColor,
        appBar: AppBar(
          backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
          elevation: 0,
          title: Text(
            'لوحة المعلم',
            style: AppTextStyles.headingMedium(context),
          ),
          actions: [
            IconButton(
              icon: const Icon(Icons.notifications_outlined),
              onPressed: () {},
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 8),
          ],
        ),
        body: RefreshIndicator(
          onRefresh: () async {
            teacherProvider.setSelectedSegment(teacherProvider.selectedSegment);
          },
          child: SingleChildScrollView(
            physics: const AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics(),
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                _buildHeader(context, authProvider),
                const SizedBox(height: 24),
                _buildKPICards(context, teacherProvider),
                const SizedBox(height: 24),

                if (authProvider.isSuperAdmin) ...[
                  _buildAdminTools(context),
                  const SizedBox(height: 24),
                ],

                _buildActionCenter(context),
                const SizedBox(height: 24),
                _buildClassOverview(context, teacherProvider),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildHeader(BuildContext context, AuthProvider authProvider) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        gradient: LinearGradient(
          colors: [
            Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary,
            Theme.of(context).colorScheme.secondary,
          ],
          begin: Alignment.topLeft,
          end: Alignment.bottomRight,
        ),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
      ),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          const CircleAvatar(
            radius: 30,
            backgroundColor: Colors.white24,
            child: Icon(Icons.person, color: Colors.white, size: 32),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 16),
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(
                  'مرحباً، المعلم!',
                  style: AppTextStyles.bodyMedium(
                    context,
                  ).copyWith(color: Colors.white70),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 4),
                Text(
                  authProvider.userName ?? 'معلم',
                  style: AppTextStyles.headingSmall(
                    context,
                  ).copyWith(color: Colors.white),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildKPICards(
    BuildContext context,
    TeacherManagementProvider provider,
  ) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        Text('مؤشرات الأداء', style: AppTextStyles.headingSmall(context)),
        const SizedBox(height: 12),
        Row(
          children: [
            Expanded(
              child: _KPICard(
                title: 'نسبة الحضور',
                value:
                    '${provider.classAttendancePercentage.toStringAsFixed(0)}%',
                icon: Icons.people_alt,
                trend: '+5%',
                isPositiveTrend: true,
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 12),
            Expanded(
              child: _KPICard(
                title: 'تسليم الواجبات',
                value:
                    '${provider.assignmentSubmissionRate.toStringAsFixed(0)}%',
                icon: Icons.assignment_turned_in,
                trend: '+12%',
                isPositiveTrend: true,
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 12),
        _KPICard(
          title: 'متوسط الدرجات',
          value: provider.overallGradeAverage.toStringAsFixed(1),
          icon: Icons.grade,
          trend: '+3%',
          isPositiveTrend: true,
          fullWidth: true,
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildAdminTools(BuildContext context) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        Text(
          'أدوات الإدارة (Super Admin)',
          style: AppTextStyles.headingSmall(
            context,
          ).copyWith(color: Colors.purpleAccent),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 12),
        Row(
          children: [
            Expanded(
              child: _ActionButton(
                icon: Icons.admin_panel_settings,
                label: 'إدارة النظام',
                color: Colors.purple,
                onTap: () => context.go('/teacher/users'),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 8),
            Expanded(
              child: _ActionButton(
                icon: Icons.analytics,
                label: 'التحليلات الشاملة',
                color: Colors.deepPurple,
                onTap: () => _showComingSoonDialog(context),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildActionCenter(BuildContext context) {
    final authProvider = context.read<AuthProvider>();
    final canUploadVideo = authProvider.canUploadVideo;
    final canAccessAttendance = authProvider.canAccessAttendance;

    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        Text('مركز الإجراءات', style: AppTextStyles.headingSmall(context)),
        const SizedBox(height: 12),
        Row(
          children: [
            Expanded(
              child: _ActionButton(
                icon: Icons.school,
                label: 'إدارة الطلاب',
                color: AppColors.primaryAccent,
                onTap: () => context.go('/teacher/users'),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 8),
            Expanded(
              child: _ActionButton(
                icon: Icons.check_circle,
                label: 'تتبع الحضور',
                color: AppColors.successColor,
                isEnabled: canAccessAttendance,
                onTap: canAccessAttendance
                    ? () => context.go('/teacher/attendance_homework')
                    : () => _showAccessDeniedDialog(context),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 8),
            Expanded(
              child: _ActionButton(
                icon: Icons.rate_review,
                label: 'تصحيح الواجبات',
                color: Colors.orange,
                isEnabled: canAccessAttendance,
                onTap: canAccessAttendance
                    ? () => _showComingSoonDialog(context)
                    : () => _showAccessDeniedDialog(context),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 8),
            Expanded(
              child: _ActionButton(
                icon: Icons.video_library,
                label: 'إدارة المحتوى',
                color: AppColors.secondaryAccent,
                isEnabled: canUploadVideo,
                onTap: canUploadVideo
                    ? () => context.go('/teacher/upload')
                    : () => _showAccessDeniedDialog(context),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  void _showAccessDeniedDialog(BuildContext context) {
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(
        backgroundColor: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.surface,
        title: const Text('تنبيه'),
        content: const Text('ليس لديك صلاحية للوصول إلى هذه الميزة'),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
            child: const Text('حسناً'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  void _showComingSoonDialog(BuildContext context) {
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(
        backgroundColor: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.surface,
        title: const Text('قريباً'),
        content: const Text('سيتم توفير هذه الميزة قريباً'),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
            child: const Text('حسناً'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildClassOverview(
    BuildContext context,
    TeacherManagementProvider provider,
  ) {
    final classData = provider.getClassOverview();

    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        Text('نظرة عامة على الفصل', style: AppTextStyles.headingSmall(context)),
        const SizedBox(height: 12),
        ...classData.map(
          (data) => _ClassOverviewItem(
            student: data['student'],
            isPresent: data['isPresent'],
            hasPendingHomework: data['hasPendingHomework'],
            onTap: () => context.go('/teacher/student/${data['student'].id}'),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

class _KPICard extends StatelessWidget {
  final String title;
  final String value;
  final IconData icon;
  final String trend;
  final bool isPositiveTrend;
  final bool fullWidth;

  const _KPICard({
    required this.title,
    required this.value,
    required this.icon,
    required this.trend,
    required this.isPositiveTrend,
    this.fullWidth = false,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final colorScheme = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;

    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: colorScheme.surface,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
        border: Border.all(color: colorScheme.outline.withValues(alpha: 0.2)),
      ),
      child: fullWidth
          ? Row(
              children: [
                _buildIconContainer(colorScheme),
                const SizedBox(width: 16),
                Expanded(
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Text(title, style: AppTextStyles.bodySmall(context)),
                      const SizedBox(height: 4),
                      Text(
                        value,
                        style: AppTextStyles.headingMedium(context).copyWith(
                          color: colorScheme.primary,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
                _buildTrendBadge(),
              ],
            )
          : Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Row(
                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                  children: [
                    _buildIconContainer(colorScheme),
                    _buildTrendBadge(),
                  ],
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                Text(title, style: AppTextStyles.bodySmall(context)),
                const SizedBox(height: 4),
                Text(
                  value,
                  style: AppTextStyles.headingMedium(context).copyWith(
                    color: colorScheme.primary,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildIconContainer(ColorScheme colorScheme) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        gradient: LinearGradient(
          colors: [colorScheme.primary, colorScheme.secondary],
          begin: Alignment.topLeft,
          end: Alignment.bottomRight,
        ),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
      ),
      child: Icon(icon, color: Colors.white, size: 24),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildTrendBadge() {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: (isPositiveTrend ? Colors.green : Colors.red).withValues(
          alpha: 0.1,
        ),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
      ),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          Icon(
            isPositiveTrend ? Icons.trending_up : Icons.trending_down,
            size: 14,
            color: isPositiveTrend ? Colors.green : Colors.red,
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 4),
          Text(
            trend,
            style: TextStyle(
              color: isPositiveTrend ? Colors.green : Colors.red,
              fontSize: 12,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _ActionButton extends StatelessWidget {
  final IconData icon;
  final String label;
  final Color color;
  final VoidCallback onTap;
  final bool isEnabled;

  const _ActionButton({
    required this.icon,
    required this.label,
    required this.color,
    required this.onTap,
    this.isEnabled = true,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final displayColor = isEnabled ? color : Colors.grey;

    return GestureDetector(
      onTap: onTap,
      child: Container(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16, horizontal: 8),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: displayColor.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
          border: Border.all(color: displayColor.withValues(alpha: 0.3)),
        ),
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            Icon(icon, color: displayColor, size: 24),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            Text(
              label,
              style: AppTextStyles.bodySmall(
                context,
              ).copyWith(color: displayColor, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
              maxLines: 1,
              overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _ClassOverviewItem extends StatelessWidget {
  final dynamic student;
  final bool isPresent;
  final bool hasPendingHomework;
  final VoidCallback onTap;

  const _ClassOverviewItem({
    required this.student,
    required this.isPresent,
    required this.hasPendingHomework,
    required this.onTap,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final colorScheme = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;

    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
      child: GestureDetector(
        onTap: onTap,
        child: Container(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: colorScheme.surface,
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
            border: Border.all(
              color: colorScheme.outline.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
            ),
          ),
          child: Row(
            children: [
              CircleAvatar(
                radius: 24,
                backgroundColor: colorScheme.primary.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                child: Icon(Icons.person, color: colorScheme.primary),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 16),
              Expanded(
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Text(
                      student.name,
                      style: AppTextStyles.bodyMedium(
                        context,
                      ).copyWith(fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 4),
                    Text('طالب', style: AppTextStyles.bodySmall(context)),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: isPresent
                      ? Colors.green.withValues(alpha: 0.1)
                      : Colors.red.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                ),
                child: Icon(
                  isPresent ? Icons.check_circle : Icons.cancel,
                  color: isPresent ? Colors.green : Colors.red,
                  size: 20,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 12),
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: hasPendingHomework
                      ? Colors.orange.withValues(alpha: 0.1)
                      : Colors.green.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                ),
                child: Icon(
                  hasPendingHomework
                      ? Icons.assignment_late
                      : Icons.assignment_turned_in,
                  color: hasPendingHomework ? Colors.orange : Colors.green,
                  size: 20,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              Icon(
                Icons.chevron_left,
                color: colorScheme.onSurface.withValues(alpha: 0.5),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

FILE: ..\edutech_app\lib\features\teacher\screens\upload_video_screen.dart

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';
import 'package:file_picker/file_picker.dart';
import '../../../core/constants/app_colors.dart';
import '../../../core/constants/app_text_styles.dart';
import '../../../core/services/video_upload_service.dart';
import '../../../core/services/admin_user_service.dart';
import '../../../shared/widgets/glass_card.dart';
import '../../../models/quiz_model.dart';
import '../../../models/api/subject_model.dart';
import 'quiz_builder_sheet.dart';

class UploadVideoScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const UploadVideoScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<UploadVideoScreen> createState() => _UploadVideoScreenState();
}

class _UploadVideoScreenState extends State<UploadVideoScreen> {
  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  final _titleController = TextEditingController();
  final _descriptionController = TextEditingController();
  final _homeworkController = TextEditingController();

  Subject? _selectedSubject;
  List<Subject> _subjectsList = [];
  bool _isLoadingSubjects = true;
  PlatformFile? _selectedVideoFile;
  PlatformFile? _selectedThumbnailFile;
  bool _isPublic = true;
  bool _hasHomework = false;
  bool _attachQuiz = false;
  bool _isUploading = false;
  double _uploadProgress = 0.0;
  QuizModel? _attachedQuiz;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _loadSubjects();
  }

  Future<void> _loadSubjects() async {
    try {
      final subjects = await AdminUserService.getSubjects();
      debugPrint('Total subjects loaded: ${subjects.length}');
      setState(() {
        _subjectsList = subjects;
        _isLoadingSubjects = false;
      });
      debugPrint(
        'Subjects list updated: ${_subjectsList.map((s) => '${s.name}(${s.id})').join(', ')}',
      );
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Error loading subjects: $e');
      setState(() {
        _isLoadingSubjects = false;
      });
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _titleController.dispose();
    _descriptionController.dispose();
    _homeworkController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _pickVideo() async {
    try {
      final result = await FilePicker.platform.pickFiles(
        type: FileType.video,
        allowMultiple: false,
        withData: true,
      );

      debugPrint('FilePicker result: ${result?.files.length} files');

      if (result != null && result.files.isNotEmpty) {
        final file = result.files.first;

        debugPrint('Selected file: ${file.name}');
        debugPrint('File size: ${file.size}');
        debugPrint('File bytes: ${file.bytes != null ? "available" : "null"}');
        debugPrint('File path: ${file.path}');

        setState(() {
          _selectedVideoFile = file;
        });

        _showSnackBar('تم اختيار الفيديو: ${file.name}', isSuccess: true);
      } else {
        debugPrint('No file selected or picker cancelled');
      }
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Error picking video: $e');
      _showSnackBar('خطأ في اختيار الفيديو: $e');
    }
  }

  Future<void> _pickCoverImage() async {
    try {
      final result = await FilePicker.platform.pickFiles(
        type: FileType.image,
        allowMultiple: false,
        withData: true,
      );

      if (result != null && result.files.isNotEmpty) {
        setState(() {
          _selectedThumbnailFile = result.files.first;
        });
        _showSnackBar('تم اختيار صورة الغلاف', isSuccess: true);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      _showSnackBar('خطأ في اختيار الصورة: $e');
    }
  }

  void _clearVideo() {
    setState(() {
      _selectedVideoFile = null;
    });
    _showSnackBar('تم إزالة الفيديو');
  }

  void _clearThumbnail() {
    setState(() {
      _selectedThumbnailFile = null;
    });
    _showSnackBar('تم إزالة صورة الغلاف');
  }

  String _formatFileSize(int? bytes) {
    if (bytes == null) return 'Unknown';
    if (bytes < 1024) return '$bytes B';
    if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) return '${(bytes / 1024).toStringAsFixed(1)} KB';
    if (bytes < 1024 * 1024 * 1024) {
      return '${(bytes / (1024 * 1024)).toStringAsFixed(1)} MB';
    }
    return '${(bytes / (1024 * 1024 * 1024)).toStringAsFixed(1)} GB';
  }

  void _showSnackBar(String message, {bool isSuccess = false}) {
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
      SnackBar(
        content: Text(message),
        backgroundColor: isSuccess ? AppColors.successColor : Colors.red,
        behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
      ),
    );
  }

  Future<void> _openQuizBuilder() async {
    final result = await showModalBottomSheet<QuizModel>(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
      builder: (context) => const QuizBuilderSheet(),
    );

    if (result != null) {
      setState(() {
        _attachedQuiz = result;
        _attachQuiz = true;
      });
      _showSnackBar(
        'تم ربط الاختبار بنجاح (${result.questionCount} سؤال)',
        isSuccess: true,
      );
    }
  }

  void _removeQuiz() {
    setState(() {
      _attachedQuiz = null;
      _attachQuiz = false;
    });
    _showSnackBar('تم إزالة الاختبار');
  }

  Future<void> _publishLesson() async {
    // Validate form fields
    if (!_formKey.currentState!.validate()) {
      _showSnackBar('يرجى إكمال جميع الحقول المطلوبة');
      return;
    }

    // Validate video selection
    if (_selectedVideoFile == null) {
      _showSnackBar('يرجى اختيار فيديو');
      return;
    }

    // Validate subject selection
    if (_selectedSubject == null) {
      _showSnackBar('يرجى اختيار المادة');
      return;
    }

    // Validate file has bytes (for web)
    if (_selectedVideoFile!.bytes == null && _selectedVideoFile!.path == null) {
      _showSnackBar('لا يمكن قراءة الملف. يرجى إعادة اختيار الفيديو');
      return;
    }

    debugPrint('=== STARTING UPLOAD ===');
    debugPrint('Title: ${_titleController.text}');
    debugPrint('Subject: $_selectedSubject');
    debugPrint(
      'Video: ${_selectedVideoFile!.name} (${_selectedVideoFile!.size} bytes)',
    );

    setState(() {
      _isUploading = true;
      _uploadProgress = 0.0;
    });

    try {
      final uploadService = VideoUploadService();
      final result = await uploadService.uploadVideo(
        title: _titleController.text,
        subjectId: _selectedSubject!.id,
        videoFile: _selectedVideoFile!,
        thumbnailFile: _selectedThumbnailFile,
        homeworkText: _hasHomework ? _homeworkController.text : null,
        onProgress: (sent, total) {
          setState(() {
            _uploadProgress = total > 0 ? sent / total : 0.0;
          });
          debugPrint(
            'Progress: ${(_uploadProgress * 100).toStringAsFixed(0)}%',
          );
        },
      );

      setState(() {
        _isUploading = false;
      });

      if (result.isSuccess) {
        _showSnackBar(result.message ?? 'تم نشر الدرس بنجاح!', isSuccess: true);
        await Future.delayed(const Duration(seconds: 1));
        if (mounted) {
          context.go('/teacher/dashboard');
        }
      } else {
        _showSnackBar(result.error ?? 'فشل رفع الفيديو');
      }
    } catch (e) {
      setState(() {
        _isUploading = false;
      });
      debugPrint('Upload error: $e');
      _showSnackBar('خطأ في الرفع: $e');
    }
  }

  Future<bool?> _showWarningDialog({
    required String title,
    required String message,
    required String confirmText,
    bool isDestructive = false,
  }) {
    return showDialog<bool>(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(
        backgroundColor: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.surface,
        title: Text(title),
        content: Text(message),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, false),
            child: const Text('إلغاء'),
          ),
          ElevatedButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, true),
            style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
              backgroundColor: isDestructive
                  ? Colors.orange
                  : AppColors.primaryAccent,
            ),
            child: Text(confirmText),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final colorScheme = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    final backgroundColor = const Color(0xFF0B101B);

    return Directionality(
      textDirection: TextDirection.rtl,
      child: Scaffold(
        backgroundColor: backgroundColor,
        appBar: AppBar(
          title: Text(
            'إنشاء درس جديد',
            style: AppTextStyles.headingMedium(context),
          ),
          backgroundColor: backgroundColor,
          elevation: 0,
          leading: IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back),
            onPressed: () => context.go('/teacher/dashboard'),
          ),
        ),
        body: Form(
          key: _formKey,
          child: SingleChildScrollView(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                _buildSectionTitle('مقاطع الفيديو'),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                _buildVideoPicker(colorScheme),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                _buildThumbnailPicker(colorScheme),
                const SizedBox(height: 24),

                _buildSectionTitle('تفاصيل الدرس'),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                _buildTitleField(),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                _buildSubjectDropdown(),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                _buildDescriptionField(),
                const SizedBox(height: 24),

                _buildSectionTitle('الواجب'),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                _buildHomeworkSection(colorScheme),
                if (_hasHomework) ...[
                  const SizedBox(height: 12),
                  _buildHomeworkField(),
                ],
                const SizedBox(height: 24),

                _buildSectionTitle('الاختبار'),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                _buildQuizSection(colorScheme),
                if (_attachQuiz && _attachedQuiz != null) ...[
                  const SizedBox(height: 12),
                  _buildAttachedQuizCard(colorScheme),
                ],
                const SizedBox(height: 24),

                _buildSectionTitle('الإعدادات'),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                _buildVisibilityToggle(colorScheme),
                const SizedBox(height: 32),

                _buildPublishButton(),
                const SizedBox(height: 24),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildSectionTitle(String title) {
    return Text(
      title,
      style: AppTextStyles.headingSmall(context).copyWith(color: Colors.white),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildVideoPicker(ColorScheme colorScheme) {
    final isSelected = _selectedVideoFile != null;

    return GestureDetector(
      onTap: _pickVideo,
      child: AnimatedContainer(
        duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 200),
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          gradient: isSelected
              ? LinearGradient(
                  colors: [
                    AppColors.successColor,
                    AppColors.successColor.withValues(alpha: 0.7),
                  ],
                  begin: Alignment.topLeft,
                  end: Alignment.bottomRight,
                )
              : LinearGradient(
                  colors: [colorScheme.primary, colorScheme.secondary],
                  begin: Alignment.topLeft,
                  end: Alignment.bottomRight,
                ),
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
          boxShadow: isSelected
              ? []
              : [
                  BoxShadow(
                    color: colorScheme.primary.withValues(alpha: 0.3),
                    blurRadius: 20,
                    offset: const Offset(0, 10),
                  ),
                ],
        ),
        child: isSelected
            ? _buildSelectedVideoContent()
            : _buildVideoPickerContent(),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildVideoPickerContent() {
    return Column(
      children: [
        Container(
          width: 80,
          height: 80,
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
          ),
          child: const Icon(Icons.video_library, size: 40, color: Colors.white),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        Text(
          'انقر لاختيار الفيديو',
          style: AppTextStyles.bodyLarge(context).copyWith(color: Colors.white),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 8),
        Container(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 6),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
          ),
          child: const Text(
            'MP4, MOV - أقصى 500MB',
            style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 12),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildSelectedVideoContent() {
    return Row(
      children: [
        Container(
          width: 80,
          height: 80,
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
          ),
          child: const Icon(
            Icons.play_circle_filled,
            size: 48,
            color: Colors.white,
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(width: 16),
        Expanded(
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              const Text(
                'تم اختيار الفيديو',
                style: TextStyle(
                  color: Colors.white,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                  fontSize: 16,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 4),
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                  horizontal: 12,
                  vertical: 4,
                ),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                ),
                child: Text(
                  _selectedVideoFile!.name,
                  style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 12),
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 4),
              Text(
                _formatFileSize(_selectedVideoFile!.size),
                style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 12),
              ),

          ),
        ),
        IconButton(
          icon: const Icon(Icons.close, color: Colors.white),
          onPressed: () => setState(() => _selectedVideoFile = null),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildThumbnailPicker(ColorScheme colorScheme) {
    final isSelected = _selectedThumbnailFile != null;

    return GestureDetector(
      onTap: _pickCoverImage,
      child: Container(
        height: 120,
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: const Color(0xFF1A2332),
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
          border: Border.all(

                ? colorScheme.primary
                : Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
            width: isSelected ? 2 : 1,

            ? _buildSelectedThumbnail()
            : Center(
                child: Column(
                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                  children: [
                    Icon(
                      Icons.add_photo_alternate_outlined,
                      size: 40,
                      color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.5),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 8),
                    Text(
                      'إضافة صورة الغلاف',
                      style: AppTextStyles.bodyMedium(
                        context,
                      ).copyWith(color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.5)),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildSelectedThumbnail() {
    return Stack(
      children: [
        ClipRRect(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
          child: Container(
            width: double.infinity,
            height: 120,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              gradient: LinearGradient(
                colors: [
                  Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary.withValues(alpha: 0.5),
                  Theme.of(
                    context,
                  ).colorScheme.secondary.withValues(alpha: 0.5),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            child: const Center(
              child: Icon(Icons.image, size: 48, color: Colors.white),
            ),
          ),
        ),
        Positioned(
          top: 8,
          right: 8,
          child: Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 6),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: AppColors.successColor,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
            ),
            child: Row(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              children: [
                const Icon(Icons.check, color: Colors.white, size: 16),
                const SizedBox(width: 4),
                Text(
                  _selectedThumbnailFile!.name,
                  style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 12),
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
        Positioned(
          top: 8,
          left: 8,
          child: IconButton(
            icon: Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(4),
              decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                color: Colors.black54,
                shape: BoxShape.circle,
              ),
              child: const Icon(Icons.close, color: Colors.white, size: 20),
            ),
            onPressed: () => setState(() => _selectedThumbnailFile = null),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildTitleField() {
    return _GlowTextField(
      controller: _titleController,
      labelText: 'عنوان الدرس',
      hintText: 'أدخل عنوان الدرس',
      prefixIcon: Icons.title,
      validator: (value) {
        if (value == null || value.isEmpty) {
          return 'يرجى إدخال عنوان الدرس';
        }
        return null;
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _buildSubjectDropdown() {
    if (_isLoadingSubjects) {
      return Container(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: const Color(0xFF1A2332),
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        ),
        child: const Row(
          children: [
            SizedBox(
              width: 20,
              height: 20,
              child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                strokeWidth: 2,
                color: Colors.white54,
              ),
            ),
            SizedBox(width: 12),
            Text(
              'جاري تحميل المواد...',
              style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white54),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      );
    }

    if (_subjectsList.isEmpty) {
      return Container(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: const Color(0xFF1A2332),
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        ),
        child: const Text(
          'لا توجد مواد متاحة',
          style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white54),
        ),
      );
    }

    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: const Color(0xFF1A2332),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        border: Border.all(color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.1)),
      ),
      child: DropdownButtonHideUnderline(
        child: DropdownButton<Subject>(
          value: _getSelectedSubject(),
          hint: const Text(
            'اختر المادة',
            style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white54),
          ),
          isExpanded: true,
          dropdownColor: const Color(0xFF1A2332),
          items: _subjectsList.map((subject) {
            return DropdownMenuItem(
              value: subject,
              child: Text(
                subject.name,
                style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white),
              ),
            );
          }).toList(),
          onChanged: (Subject? value) {
            if (value != null) {
              setState(() {
                _selectedSubject = value;
              });
            }
          },
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Subject? _getSelectedSubject() {
    if (_selectedSubject == null || _subjectsList.isEmpty) return null;
    try {
      return _subjectsList.firstWhere((s) => s.id == _selectedSubject!.id);
    } catch (_) {
      return null;
    }
  }

  Widget _buildDescriptionField() {
    return _GlowTextField(
      controller: _descriptionController,
      labelText: 'الوصف',
      hintText: 'أدخل وصف الدرس',
      prefixIcon: Icons.description_outlined,
      maxLines: 3,
    );
  }

  Widget _buildHomeworkSection(ColorScheme colorScheme) {
    return GlassCard(
      child: Row(
        children: [
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: AppColors.successColor.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
            ),
            child: const Icon(Icons.assignment, color: AppColors.successColor),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 16),
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(
                  'إضافة واجب',
                  style: AppTextStyles.bodyMedium(
                    context,
                  ).copyWith(fontWeight: FontWeight.w600, color: Colors.white),
                ),
                Text(
                  'إنشاء واجب للطلاب',
                  style: AppTextStyles.bodySmall(
                    context,
                  ).copyWith(color: Colors.white54),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          Switch(
            value: _hasHomework,
            onChanged: (value) => setState(() => _hasHomework = value),
            activeThumbColor: AppColors.successColor,
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildHomeworkField() {
    return _GlowTextField(
      controller: _homeworkController,
      labelText: 'تعليمات الواجب',
      hintText: 'أدخل تعليمات الواجب',
      prefixIcon: Icons.assignment_outlined,
      maxLines: 4,
    );
  }

  Widget _buildQuizSection(ColorScheme colorScheme) {
    return GlassCard(
      child: Row(
        children: [
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: Colors.orange.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
            ),
            child: const Icon(Icons.quiz, color: Colors.orange),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 16),
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(
                  'ربط اختبار',
                  style: AppTextStyles.bodyMedium(
                    context,
                  ).copyWith(fontWeight: FontWeight.w600, color: Colors.white),
                ),
                Text(
                  'أضف اختبار للدرس',
                  style: AppTextStyles.bodySmall(
                    context,
                  ).copyWith(color: Colors.white54),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          Switch(
            value: _attachQuiz,
            onChanged: (value) {
              setState(() => _attachQuiz = value);
              if (value && _attachedQuiz == null) {
                _openQuizBuilder();
              } else if (!value) {
                _removeQuiz();
              }
            },
            activeThumbColor: Colors.orange,
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildAttachedQuizCard(ColorScheme colorScheme) {
    return AnimatedContainer(
      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        gradient: LinearGradient(
          colors: [
            AppColors.successColor.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
            AppColors.successColor.withValues(alpha: 0.05),
          ],
        ),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
        border: Border.all(
          color: AppColors.successColor.withValues(alpha: 0.3),
        ),
      ),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: AppColors.successColor.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
            ),
            child: const Icon(
              Icons.check_circle,
              color: AppColors.successColor,
              size: 28,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 16),
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(
                  'الاختبار مرفق',
                  style: AppTextStyles.bodyMedium(
                    context,
                  ).copyWith(fontWeight: FontWeight.w600, color: Colors.white),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 4),
                Text(
                  '${_attachedQuiz!.questionCount} سؤال',
                  style: AppTextStyles.bodySmall(
                    context,
                  ).copyWith(color: Colors.white70),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          Row(
            children: [
              IconButton(
                icon: const Icon(Icons.edit, color: Colors.orange),
                onPressed: _openQuizBuilder,
              ),
              IconButton(
                icon: const Icon(Icons.delete_outline, color: Colors.red),
                onPressed: _removeQuiz,
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildVisibilityToggle(ColorScheme colorScheme) {
    return GlassCard(
      child: Row(
        children: [
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: colorScheme.primary.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
            ),
            child: Icon(
              _isPublic ? Icons.public : Icons.lock,
              color: colorScheme.primary,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 16),
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(
                  _isPublic ? 'عام' : 'خاص',
                  style: AppTextStyles.bodyMedium(
                    context,
                  ).copyWith(fontWeight: FontWeight.w600, color: Colors.white),
                ),
                Text(
                  _isPublic ? 'يمكن للجميع المشاهدة' : 'للطلاب المسجلين فقط',
                  style: AppTextStyles.bodySmall(
                    context,
                  ).copyWith(color: Colors.white54),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          Switch(
            value: _isPublic,
            onChanged: (value) => setState(() => _isPublic = value),
            activeTrackColor: colorScheme.primary,
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildPublishButton() {
    return SizedBox(
      width: double.infinity,
      height: 56,
      child: Container(
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          gradient: LinearGradient(
            colors: [
              Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary,
              Theme.of(context).colorScheme.secondary,
            ],
            begin: Alignment.centerLeft,
            end: Alignment.centerRight,
          ),
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
          boxShadow: [
            BoxShadow(
              color: Theme.of(
                context,
              ).colorScheme.primary.withValues(alpha: 0.4),
              blurRadius: 20,
              offset: const Offset(0, 10),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        child: ElevatedButton(
          onPressed: _publishLesson,
          style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
            backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
            shadowColor: Colors.transparent,
            shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
            ),
          ),
          child: Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
            children: [
              const Icon(Icons.publish, color: Colors.white),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              Text(
                'نشر الدرس',
                style: AppTextStyles.bodyLarge(
                  context,
                ).copyWith(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _GlowTextField extends StatelessWidget {
  final TextEditingController controller;
  final String labelText;
  final String hintText;
  final IconData prefixIcon;
  final int maxLines;
  final String? Function(String?)? validator;

  const _GlowTextField({
    required this.controller,
    required this.labelText,
    required this.hintText,
    required this.prefixIcon,
    this.maxLines = 1,
    this.validator,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Focus(
      onFocusChange: (hasFocus) {},
      child: Container(
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
          boxShadow: [],
        ),
        child: TextFormField(
          controller: controller,
          maxLines: maxLines,
          style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white),
          validator: validator,
          decoration: InputDecoration(
            labelText: labelText,
            hintText: hintText,
            prefixIcon: Icon(prefixIcon, color: Colors.white54),
            filled: true,
            fillColor: const Color(0xFF1A2332),
            hintStyle: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white38),
            labelStyle: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white70),
            border: OutlineInputBorder(
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
              borderSide: BorderSide(
                color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
              ),
            ),
            enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
              borderSide: BorderSide(
                color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
              ),
            ),
            focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
              borderSide: BorderSide(
                color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary,
                width: 2,
              ),
            ),
            errorBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
              borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Colors.red),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
