import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';
import '../../../core/constants/app_colors.dart';
import '../../../core/constants/app_text_styles.dart';
import '../../../core/providers/auth_provider.dart';
import '../../../core/providers/teacher_management_provider.dart';
import '../../../shared/widgets/glass_card.dart';

class UserDirectoryScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const UserDirectoryScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<UserDirectoryScreen> createState() => _UserDirectoryScreenState();
}

class _UserDirectoryScreenState extends State<UserDirectoryScreen> {
  final TextEditingController _searchController = TextEditingController();
  final FocusNode _searchFocusNode = FocusNode();

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _searchController.addListener(_onSearchChanged);
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _searchController.removeListener(_onSearchChanged);
    _searchController.dispose();
    _searchFocusNode.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _onSearchChanged() {
    final provider = context.read<TeacherManagementProvider>();
    provider.setSearchQuery(_searchController.text);
  }

  void _clearSearch() {
    _searchController.clear();
    _searchFocusNode.unfocus();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Directionality(
      textDirection: TextDirection.rtl,
      child: Scaffold(
        backgroundColor: AppColors.backgroundDark,
        appBar: AppBar(
          backgroundColor: AppColors.backgroundDark,
          elevation: 0,
          title: Text(
            'دليل المستخدمين',
            style: AppTextStyles.headingMedium(context),
          ),
          leading: IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back),
            onPressed: () => context.go('/teacher/dashboard'),
          ),
        ),
        body: Consumer<TeacherManagementProvider>(
          builder: (context, provider, child) {
            final currentUser = context.read<AuthProvider>().getCurrentUser();
            final searchResults = provider.getSearchResults(currentUser);

            return Column(
              children: [
                Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                  child: Column(
                    children: [
                      _buildSearchBar(),
                      const SizedBox(height: 12),
                      _FilterChips(
                        currentFilter: provider.currentFilter,
                        onFilterChanged: provider.setFilter,
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
                Expanded(
                  child: searchResults.isEmpty
                      ? _buildEmptyState()
                      : ListView.builder(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16),
                          itemCount: searchResults.length,
                          itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                            final user = searchResults[index];
                            return _UserCard(
                              name: user.name,
                              role: user.role,
                              phoneNumber: user.phoneNumber,
                              parentPhoneNumber: user.parentPhoneNumber,
                              isApproved: user.isApproved,
                              isSuperAdmin: context
                                  .read<AuthProvider>()
                                  .isSuperAdmin,
                              onTap: user.role == 'student'
                                  ? () => context.go(
                                      '/teacher/student/${user.id}',
                                    )
                                  : null,
                              onApprovalToggle:
                                  context.read<AuthProvider>().isSuperAdmin &&
                                      user.role == 'pendingTeacher'
                                  ? () {
                                      context
                                          .read<AuthProvider>()
                                          .updateUserApproval(
                                            user.phoneNumber,
                                            !user.isApproved,
                                          );
                                    }
                                  : null,
                              onRoleChange:
                                  context.read<AuthProvider>().isSuperAdmin
                                  ? (newRole) {
                                      context
                                          .read<AuthProvider>()
                                          .updateUserRole(
                                            user.phoneNumber,
                                            newRole,
                                          );
                                    }
                                  : null,
                            );
                          },
                        ),
                ),
              ],
            );
          },
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildSearchBar() {
    return Container(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: AppColors.surfaceDark,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        border: Border.all(
          color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.outline.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
        ),
      ),
      child: TextField(
        controller: _searchController,
        focusNode: _searchFocusNode,
        style: AppTextStyles.bodyMedium(context),
        decoration: InputDecoration(
          hintText: 'البحث عن طالب أو معلم...',
          hintStyle: AppTextStyles.bodyMedium(
            context,
          ).copyWith(color: AppColors.textSecondaryDark),
          prefixIcon: const Icon(
            Icons.search,
            color: AppColors.textSecondaryDark,
          ),
          suffixIcon: _searchController.text.isNotEmpty
              ? IconButton(
                  icon: const Icon(
                    Icons.clear,
                    color: AppColors.textSecondaryDark,
                  ),
                  onPressed: _clearSearch,
                )
              : null,
          border: InputBorder.none,
          contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
            horizontal: 16,
            vertical: 14,
          ),
        ),
        onChanged: (value) {
          setState(() {});
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildEmptyState() {
    return Center(
      child: Column(
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
        children: [
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: AppColors.surfaceDark,
              shape: BoxShape.circle,
            ),
            child: Icon(
              Icons.search_off,
              size: 64,
              color: AppColors.textSecondaryDark.withValues(alpha: 0.5),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),
          Text(
            'لا توجد نتائج',
            style: AppTextStyles.headingSmall(
              context,
            ).copyWith(color: AppColors.textSecondaryDark),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Text(
            'لم يتم العثور على طلاب مطابقين للبحث',
            style: AppTextStyles.bodyMedium(
              context,
            ).copyWith(color: AppColors.textSecondaryDark),
            textAlign: TextAlign.center,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),
          TextButton.icon(
            onPressed: _clearSearch,
            icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh),
            label: const Text('مسح البحث'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _FilterChips extends StatelessWidget {
  final UserFilter currentFilter;
  final Function(UserFilter) onFilterChanged;

  const _FilterChips({
    required this.currentFilter,
    required this.onFilterChanged,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return SingleChildScrollView(
      scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
      child: Row(
        children: [
          _FilterChip(
            label: 'الكل',
            isSelected: currentFilter == UserFilter.all,
            onTap: () => onFilterChanged(UserFilter.all),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 8),
          _FilterChip(
            label: 'الطلاب',
            isSelected: currentFilter == UserFilter.students,
            onTap: () => onFilterChanged(UserFilter.students),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 8),
          _FilterChip(
            label: 'المعلمون',
            isSelected: currentFilter == UserFilter.teachers,
            onTap: () => onFilterChanged(UserFilter.teachers),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 8),
          _FilterChip(
            label: 'المعلمون بانتظار التفعيل',
            isSelected: currentFilter == UserFilter.pendingTeachers,
            onTap: () => onFilterChanged(UserFilter.pendingTeachers),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 8),
          _FilterChip(
            label: 'أولياء الأمور',
            isSelected: currentFilter == UserFilter.parents,
            onTap: () => onFilterChanged(UserFilter.parents),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _FilterChip extends StatelessWidget {
  final String label;
  final bool isSelected;
  final VoidCallback onTap;

  const _FilterChip({
    required this.label,
    required this.isSelected,
    required this.onTap,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return GestureDetector(
      onTap: onTap,
      child: Container(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20, vertical: 10),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: isSelected
              ? Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary
              : AppColors.surfaceDark,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
          border: Border.all(
            color: isSelected
                ? Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary
                : AppColors.textSecondaryDark,
          ),
        ),
        child: Text(
          label,
          style: AppTextStyles.bodyMedium(context).copyWith(
            color: isSelected ? Colors.white : AppColors.textSecondaryDark,
            fontWeight: isSelected ? FontWeight.bold : FontWeight.normal,
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _UserCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final String name;
  final String role;
  final String phoneNumber;
  final String? parentPhoneNumber;
  final bool isApproved;
  final VoidCallback? onTap;
  final bool isSuperAdmin;
  final VoidCallback? onApprovalToggle;
  final Function(String)? onRoleChange;

  const _UserCard({
    required this.name,
    required this.role,
    required this.phoneNumber,
    this.parentPhoneNumber,
    this.isApproved = true,
    this.onTap,
    this.isSuperAdmin = false,
    this.onApprovalToggle,
    this.onRoleChange,
  });

  IconData _getRoleIcon() {
    switch (role) {
      case 'student':
        return Icons.school;
      case 'teacher':
        return Icons.person;
      case 'parent':
        return Icons.family_restroom;
      case 'superAdmin':
        return Icons.admin_panel_settings;
      case 'supervisor':
        return Icons.supervisor_account;
      case 'assistant':
        return Icons.assistant;
      case 'pendingTeacher':
        return Icons.pending_actions;
      default:
        return Icons.person;
    }
  }

  Color _getRoleColor() {
    switch (role) {
      case 'student':
        return AppColors.primaryAccent;
      case 'teacher':
        return AppColors.secondaryAccent;
      case 'parent':
        return AppColors.successColor;
      case 'superAdmin':
        return Colors.purple;
      case 'supervisor':
        return Colors.orange;
      case 'assistant':
        return Colors.teal;
      case 'pendingTeacher':
        return Colors.red;
      default:
        return AppColors.textSecondaryDark;
    }
  }

  String _getRoleLabel() {
    switch (role) {
      case 'student':
        return 'طالب';
      case 'teacher':
        return 'معلم';
      case 'parent':
        return 'ولي أمر';
      case 'superAdmin':
        return 'مدير عام';
      case 'supervisor':
        return 'مشرف';
      case 'assistant':
        return 'مساعد';
      case 'pendingTeacher':
        return 'معلم بانتظار التفعيل';
      default:
        return role;
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final roleColor = _getRoleColor();

    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
      child: GestureDetector(
        onTap: onTap,
        onLongPress:
            isSuperAdmin && (role == 'teacher' || role == 'pendingTeacher')
            ? () => _showRoleChangeDialog(context)
            : null,
        child: GlassCard(
          child: Row(
            children: [
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: roleColor.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                ),
                child: Icon(_getRoleIcon(), color: roleColor, size: 28),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 16),
              Expanded(
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Row(
                      children: [
                        Expanded(
                          child: Text(
                            name,
                            style: AppTextStyles.bodyMedium(
                              context,
                            ).copyWith(fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
                          ),
                        ),
                        Container(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                            horizontal: 8,
                            vertical: 4,
                          ),
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color: roleColor.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                          ),
                          child: Text(
                            _getRoleLabel(),
                            style: AppTextStyles.bodySmall(context).copyWith(
                              color: roleColor,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 4),
                    Text(phoneNumber, style: AppTextStyles.bodySmall(context)),
                    if (parentPhoneNumber != null) ...[
                      const SizedBox(height: 2),
                      Text(
                        'ولي الأمر: $parentPhoneNumber',
                        style: AppTextStyles.bodySmall(
                          context,
                        ).copyWith(color: AppColors.textSecondaryDark),
                      ),
                    ],
                    if (role == 'pendingTeacher' && isSuperAdmin) ...[
                      const SizedBox(height: 8),
                      Row(
                        children: [
                          Text(
                            'مفعل: ',
                            style: AppTextStyles.bodySmall(context),
                          ),
                          Switch(
                            value: isApproved,
                            onChanged: onApprovalToggle != null
                                ? (_) => onApprovalToggle!()
                                : null,
                            activeThumbColor: AppColors.successColor,
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ],
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              if (onTap != null)
                const Icon(
                  Icons.chevron_left,
                  color: AppColors.textSecondaryDark,
                ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  void _showRoleChangeDialog(BuildContext context) {
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(
        backgroundColor: AppColors.surfaceDark,
        title: Text('تغيير الدور', style: AppTextStyles.headingSmall(context)),
        content: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            Text(
              'اختر الدور الجديد للمستخدم $name',
              style: AppTextStyles.bodyMedium(context),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            if (role != 'supervisor')
              ListTile(
                leading: const Icon(
                  Icons.supervisor_account,
                  color: Colors.orange,
                ),
                title: const Text('مشرف'),
                onTap: () {
                  onRoleChange?.call('supervisor');
                  Navigator.pop(context);
                },
              ),
            if (role != 'assistant')
              ListTile(
                leading: const Icon(Icons.assistant, color: Colors.teal),
                title: const Text('مساعد'),
                onTap: () {
                  onRoleChange?.call('assistant');
                  Navigator.pop(context);
                },
              ),
            if (role != 'teacher')
              ListTile(
                leading: const Icon(
                  Icons.person,
                  color: AppColors.secondaryAccent,
                ),
                title: const Text('معلم'),
                onTap: () {
                  onRoleChange?.call('teacher');
                  Navigator.pop(context);
                },
              ),
          ],
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
            child: const Text('إلغاء'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';
import 'core/theme/app_theme.dart';
import 'core/router/app_router.dart';
import 'core/providers/auth_provider.dart';
import 'core/providers/theme_provider.dart';
import 'core/providers/teacher_management_provider.dart';
import 'core/services/api_service.dart';
import 'core/services/auth_service.dart';

void main() async {
  WidgetsFlutterBinding.ensureInitialized();

  // Set up API service callbacks for error handling
  _setupApiCallbacks();

  final authProvider = AuthProvider();
  await authProvider.initialize();

  final themeProvider = ThemeProvider();
  await themeProvider.initialize();

  runApp(
    MultiProvider(
      providers: [
        ChangeNotifierProvider.value(value: authProvider),
        ChangeNotifierProvider.value(value: themeProvider),
        ChangeNotifierProvider(create: (_) => TeacherManagementProvider()),
      ],
      child: const EduTechApp(),
    ),
  );
}

void _setupApiCallbacks() {
  // Handle 401 Unauthorized - Token expired or invalid
  ApiService.onUnauthorized = () async {
    // Clear all auth data
    await AuthService.clearAllData();

    // Navigate to login
    navigatorKey.currentState?.pushNamedAndRemoveUntil(
      '/login',
      (route) => false,
    );
  };

  // Handle 403 Forbidden
  ApiService.onForbidden = (message) {
    debugPrint('Access forbidden: $message');
  };

  // Handle server errors
  ApiService.onServerError = () {
    debugPrint('Server error occurred');
  };
}

class EduTechApp extends StatelessWidget {
  const EduTechApp({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final authProvider = context.watch<AuthProvider>();
    final themeProvider = context.watch<ThemeProvider>();

    if (!authProvider.isInitialized || !themeProvider.isInitialized) {
      return const MaterialApp(
        home: Scaffold(body: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())),
      );
    }

    return MaterialApp.router(
      title: 'EduTech',
      debugShowCheckedModeBanner: false,
      theme: AppTheme.lightTheme,
      darkTheme: AppTheme.darkTheme,
      themeMode: themeProvider.themeMode,
      routerConfig: appRouter,
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import '../../core/config/env_config.dart';

class Subject {
  final String id;
  final String teacherId;
  final String name;
  final String code;

  Subject({
    required this.id,
    required this.teacherId,
    required this.name,
    required this.code,
  });

  factory Subject.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return Subject(
      id: json['id']?.toString() ?? '',
      teacherId: json['teacherId']?.toString() ?? '',
      name: json['name']?.toString() ?? '',
      code: json['code']?.toString() ?? '',
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() {
    return {'id': id, 'teacherId': teacherId, 'name': name, 'code': code};
  }
}

class Teacher {
  final String id;
  final String name;

  Teacher({required this.id, required this.name});

  factory Teacher.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return Teacher(
      id: json['id']?.toString() ?? '',
      name: json['name']?.toString() ?? '',
    );
  }
}

class Lesson {
  final String id;
  final String subjectId;
  final String teacherId;
  final String title;
  final String? _videoUrl;
  final String? _thumbnailUrl;
  final String? homeworkText;
  final Subject? subject;
  final Teacher? teacher;

  Lesson({
    required this.id,
    required this.subjectId,
    required this.teacherId,
    required this.title,
    String? videoUrl,
    String? thumbnailUrl,
    this.homeworkText,
    this.subject,
    this.teacher,
  }) : _videoUrl = videoUrl,
       _thumbnailUrl = thumbnailUrl;

  String get videoUrl {
    if (_videoUrl == null || _videoUrl.isEmpty) return '';
    if (_videoUrl.startsWith('http')) return _videoUrl;
    return 'http://localhost:5000$_videoUrl';
  }

  String get thumbnailUrl {
    if (_thumbnailUrl == null || _thumbnailUrl.isEmpty) return '';
    if (_thumbnailUrl.startsWith('http')) return _thumbnailUrl;
    return 'http://localhost:5000$_thumbnailUrl';
  }

  factory Lesson.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    Subject? subject;
    Teacher? teacher;

    if (json['subject'] != null) {
      try {
        subject = Subject.fromJson(json['subject'] as Map<String, dynamic>);
      } catch (_) {
        subject = null;
      }
    }

    if (json['teacher'] != null) {
      try {
        teacher = Teacher.fromJson(json['teacher'] as Map<String, dynamic>);
      } catch (_) {
        teacher = null;
      }
    }

    return Lesson(
      id: json['id']?.toString() ?? '',
      subjectId: json['subjectId']?.toString() ?? '',
      teacherId: json['teacherId']?.toString() ?? '',
      title: json['title']?.toString() ?? '',
      videoUrl: json['videoUrl']?.toString(),
      thumbnailUrl: json['thumbnailUrl']?.toString(),
      homeworkText: json['homeworkText']?.toString(),
      subject: subject,
      teacher: teacher,
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() {
    return {
      'id': id,
      'subjectId': subjectId,
      'teacherId': teacherId,
      'title': title,
      'videoUrl': _videoUrl,
      'thumbnailUrl': _thumbnailUrl,
      'homeworkText': homeworkText,
      'subject': subject?.toJson(),
    };
  }

  Lesson copyWith({
    String? id,
    String? subjectId,
    String? teacherId,
    String? title,
    String? videoUrl,
    String? thumbnailUrl,
    String? homeworkText,
    Subject? subject,
  }) {
    return Lesson(
      id: id ?? this.id,
      subjectId: subjectId ?? this.subjectId,
      teacherId: teacherId ?? this.teacherId,
      title: title ?? this.title,
      videoUrl: videoUrl ?? _videoUrl,
      thumbnailUrl: thumbnailUrl ?? _thumbnailUrl,
      homeworkText: homeworkText ?? this.homeworkText,
      subject: subject ?? this.subject,
    );
  }

  bool get hasVideo => _videoUrl != null && _videoUrl.isNotEmpty;
  bool get hasHomework => homeworkText != null && homeworkText!.isNotEmpty;
}

class Quiz {
  final String id;
  final String lessonId;
  final List<QuizQuestion> questions;

  Quiz({required this.id, required this.lessonId, required this.questions});

  factory Quiz.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    List<QuizQuestion> questions = [];

    try {
      if (json['questions'] != null) {
        if (json['questions'] is String) {
          questions = _parseQuestions(json['questions'] as String);
        } else if (json['questions'] is List) {
          questions = (json['questions'] as List)
              .map((q) => QuizQuestion.fromJson(q as Map<String, dynamic>))
              .toList();
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {
      questions = [];
    }

    return Quiz(
      id: json['id']?.toString() ?? '',
      lessonId: json['lessonId']?.toString() ?? '',
      questions: questions,
    );
  }

  static List<QuizQuestion> _parseQuestions(String jsonString) {
    try {
      final decoded = _jsonDecode(jsonString);
      if (decoded is List) {
        return decoded
            .map((q) => QuizQuestion.fromJson(q as Map<String, dynamic>))
            .toList();
      }
      return [];
    } catch (e) {
      return [];
    }
  }

  static dynamic _jsonDecode(String source) {
    return _JsonDecoder(source).decodeValue();
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() {
    return {
      'id': id,
      'lessonId': lessonId,
      'questions': questions.map((q) => q.toJson()).toList(),
    };
  }

  bool get hasQuestions => questions.isNotEmpty;
  int get totalQuestions => questions.length;
}

class QuizQuestion {
  final String question;
  final List<String> options;
  final int correctAnswer;

  QuizQuestion({
    required this.question,
    required this.options,
    required this.correctAnswer,
  });

  factory QuizQuestion.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    List<String> options = [];
    try {
      if (json['options'] is List) {
        options = (json['options'] as List)
            .map((e) => e?.toString() ?? '')
            .toList();
      }
    } catch (_) {
      options = [];
    }

    return QuizQuestion(
      question: json['question']?.toString() ?? '',
      options: options,
      correctAnswer: json['correctAnswer'] is int
          ? json['correctAnswer'] as int
          : int.tryParse(json['correctAnswer']?.toString() ?? '') ?? 0,
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() {
    return {
      'question': question,
      'options': options,
      'correctAnswer': correctAnswer,
    };
  }

  bool isCorrect(int selectedIndex) => selectedIndex == correctAnswer;
}

class _JsonDecoder {
  final String source;
  int _index = 0;

  _JsonDecoder(this.source);

  dynamic decodeValue() {
    _skipWhitespace();
    if (_isAtEnd) return null;

    final char = _peek();
    if (char == '{') return decodeObject();
    if (char == '[') return decodeArray();
    if (char == '"') return decodeString();
    if (char == 't' || char == 'f') return decodeBool();
    if (char == 'n') return decodeNull();
    if (char == '-' || _isDigit(char)) return decodeNumber();
    return null;
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> decodeObject() {
    final result = <String, dynamic>{};
    _expect('{');
    _skipWhitespace();
    if (_peek() == '}') {
      _consume();
      return result;
    }
    while (true) {
      _skipWhitespace();
      final key = decodeString();
      _skipWhitespace();
      _expect(':');
      _skipWhitespace();
      result[key] = decodeValue();
      _skipWhitespace();
      if (_peek() == ',') {
        _consume();
      } else {
        break;
      }
    }
    _expect('}');
    return result;
  }

  List<dynamic> decodeArray() {
    final result = <dynamic>[];
    _expect('[');
    _skipWhitespace();
    if (_peek() == ']') {
      _consume();
      return result;
    }
    while (true) {
      _skipWhitespace();
      result.add(decodeValue());
      _skipWhitespace();
      if (_peek() == ',') {
        _consume();
      } else {
        break;
      }
    }
    _expect(']');
    return result;
  }

  String decodeString() {
    _expect('"');
    final buffer = StringBuffer();
    while (_peek() != '"' && !_isAtEnd) {
      if (_peek() == '\\') {
        _consume();
        final escaped = _consume();
        switch (escaped) {
          case 'n':
            buffer.write('\n');
            break;
          case 't':
            buffer.write('\t');
            break;
          case 'r':
            buffer.write('\r');
            break;
          case '\\':
            buffer.write('\\');
            break;
          case '"':
            buffer.write('"');
            break;
          default:
            buffer.write(escaped);
        }
      } else {
        buffer.write(_consume());
      }
    }
    _expect('"');
    return buffer.toString();
  }

  num decodeNumber() {
    final start = _index;
    if (_peek() == '-') _consume();
    while (!_isAtEnd && _isDigit(_peek())) {
      _consume();
    }
    if (_peek() == '.') {
      _consume();
      while (!_isAtEnd && _isDigit(_peek())) {
        _consume();
      }
    }
    if (!_isAtEnd && (_peek() == 'e' || _peek() == 'E')) {
      _consume();
      if (!_isAtEnd && (_peek() == '+' || _peek() == '-')) _consume();
      while (!_isAtEnd && _isDigit(_peek())) {
        _consume();
      }
    }
    final numStr = source.substring(start, _index);
    return numStr.contains('.') ? double.parse(numStr) : int.parse(numStr);
  }

  bool decodeBool() {
    if (source.substring(_index).startsWith('true')) {
      _index += 4;
      return true;
    } else if (source.substring(_index).startsWith('false')) {
      _index += 5;
      return false;
    }
    return false;
  }

  dynamic decodeNull() {
    if (source.substring(_index).startsWith('null')) {
      _index += 4;
      return null;
    }
    return null;
  }

  void _skipWhitespace() {
    while (!_isAtEnd && ' \t\n\r'.contains(_peek())) {
      _index++;
    }
  }

  bool get _isAtEnd => _index >= source.length;
  String _peek() => _isAtEnd ? '' : source[_index];
  String _consume() => _isAtEnd ? '' : source[_index++];
  void _expect(String char) {
    if (_peek() != char) return;
    _consume();
  }

  bool _isDigit(String char) =>
      char.isNotEmpty && char.codeUnitAt(0) >= 48 && char.codeUnitAt(0) <= 57;
}

class Subject {
  final String id;
  final String teacherId;
  final String name;
  final String code;

  Subject({
    required this.id,
    required this.teacherId,
    required this.name,
    required this.code,
  });

  factory Subject.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return Subject(
      id: json['id'] as String,
      teacherId: json['teacherId'] as String,
      name: json['name'] as String,
      code: json['code'] as String,
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() {
    return {'id': id, 'teacherId': teacherId, 'name': name, 'code': code};
  }

  @override
  bool operator ==(Object other) {
    if (identical(this, other)) return true;
    return other is Subject && other.id == id;
  }

  @override
  int get hashCode => id.hashCode;
}

FILE: ..\edutech_app\lib\models\api\tracking_model.dart

enum HWStatus {
  notSubmitted,
  submitted,
  graded;

  static HWStatus fromString(String? value) {
    if (value == null) return HWStatus.notSubmitted;
    switch (value.toUpperCase()) {
      case 'SUBMITTED':
        return HWStatus.submitted;
      case 'GRADED':
        return HWStatus.graded;
      default:
        return HWStatus.notSubmitted;
    }
  }

  String toJsonString() {
    return name.toUpperCase();
  }
}

class Tracking {
  final String id;
  final String studentId;
  final String lessonId;
  final bool isPresent;
  final HWStatus hwStatus;
  final int? quizScore;
  final DateTime? createdAt;
  final DateTime? updatedAt;

  Tracking({
    required this.id,
    required this.studentId,
    required this.lessonId,
    required this.isPresent,
    required this.hwStatus,
    this.quizScore,
    this.createdAt,
    this.updatedAt,
  });

  factory Tracking.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return Tracking(
      id: json['id']?.toString() ?? '',
      studentId: json['studentId']?.toString() ?? '',
      lessonId: json['lessonId']?.toString() ?? '',
      isPresent: json['isPresent'] == true,
      hwStatus: HWStatus.fromString(json['hwStatus']?.toString()),
      quizScore: json['quizScore'] is int
          ? json['quizScore'] as int
          : int.tryParse(json['quizScore']?.toString() ?? ''),
      createdAt: _parseDateTime(json['createdAt']),
      updatedAt: _parseDateTime(json['updatedAt']),
    );
  }

  static DateTime? _parseDateTime(dynamic value) {
    if (value == null) return null;
    if (value is DateTime) return value;
    if (value is String) {
      try {
        return DateTime.parse(value);
      } catch (_) {
        return null;
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() {
    return {
      'id': id,
      'studentId': studentId,
      'lessonId': lessonId,
      'isPresent': isPresent,
      'hwStatus': hwStatus.toJsonString(),
      'quizScore': quizScore,
      'createdAt': createdAt?.toIso8601String(),
      'updatedAt': updatedAt?.toIso8601String(),
    };
  }

  Tracking copyWith({
    String? id,
    String? studentId,
    String? lessonId,
    bool? isPresent,
    HWStatus? hwStatus,
    int? quizScore,
    DateTime? createdAt,
    DateTime? updatedAt,
  }) {
    return Tracking(
      id: id ?? this.id,
      studentId: studentId ?? this.studentId,
      lessonId: lessonId ?? this.lessonId,
      isPresent: isPresent ?? this.isPresent,
      hwStatus: hwStatus ?? this.hwStatus,
      quizScore: quizScore ?? this.quizScore,
      createdAt: createdAt ?? this.createdAt,
      updatedAt: updatedAt ?? this.updatedAt,
    );
  }

  bool get hasQuizScore => quizScore != null;
  bool get isGraded => hwStatus == HWStatus.graded;
}

class QuizResult {
  final int score;
  final int totalQuestions;
  final int correctAnswers;
  final Tracking tracking;

  QuizResult({
    required this.score,
    required this.totalQuestions,
    required this.correctAnswers,
    required this.tracking,
  });

  factory QuizResult.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return QuizResult(
      score: json['score'] is int
          ? json['score'] as int
          : int.tryParse(json['score']?.toString() ?? '') ?? 0,
      totalQuestions: json['totalQuestions'] is int
          ? json['totalQuestions'] as int
          : int.tryParse(json['totalQuestions']?.toString() ?? '') ?? 0,
      correctAnswers: json['correctAnswers'] is int
          ? json['correctAnswers'] as int
          : int.tryParse(json['correctAnswers']?.toString() ?? '') ?? 0,
      tracking: Tracking.fromJson(
        json['tracking'] as Map<String, dynamic>? ?? {},
      ),
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() {
    return {
      'score': score,
      'totalQuestions': totalQuestions,
      'correctAnswers': correctAnswers,
      'tracking': tracking.toJson(),
    };
  }

  bool get isPassed => score >= 60;
}

class User {
  final String id;
  final String name;
  final String phone;
  final String role;
  final bool isApproved;
  final bool isVerified;
  final String? subjectId;
  final DateTime? createdAt;
  final DateTime? updatedAt;

  User({
    required this.id,
    required this.name,
    required this.phone,
    required this.role,
    required this.isApproved,
    required this.isVerified,
    this.subjectId,
    this.createdAt,
    this.updatedAt,
  });

  factory User.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return User(
      id: json['id']?.toString() ?? '',
      name: json['name']?.toString() ?? '',
      phone: json['phone']?.toString() ?? '',
      role: json['role']?.toString() ?? 'STUDENT',
      isApproved: json['isApproved'] == true,
      isVerified: json['isVerified'] == true,
      subjectId: json['subjectId']?.toString(),
      createdAt: json['createdAt'] != null
          ? _parseDateTime(json['createdAt'])
          : null,
      updatedAt: json['updatedAt'] != null
          ? _parseDateTime(json['updatedAt'])
          : null,
    );
  }

  static DateTime? _parseDateTime(dynamic value) {
    if (value == null) return null;
    if (value is DateTime) return value;
    if (value is String) {
      try {
        return DateTime.parse(value);
      } catch (_) {
        return null;
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() {
    return {
      'id': id,
      'name': name,
      'phone': phone,
      'role': role,
      'isApproved': isApproved,
      'isVerified': isVerified,
      'subjectId': subjectId,
      'createdAt': createdAt?.toIso8601String(),
      'updatedAt': updatedAt?.toIso8601String(),
    };
  }

  User copyWith({
    String? id,
    String? name,
    String? phone,
    String? role,
    bool? isApproved,
    bool? isVerified,
    String? subjectId,
    DateTime? createdAt,
    DateTime? updatedAt,
  }) {
    return User(
      id: id ?? this.id,
      name: name ?? this.name,
      phone: phone ?? this.phone,
      role: role ?? this.role,
      isApproved: isApproved ?? this.isApproved,
      isVerified: isVerified ?? this.isVerified,
      subjectId: subjectId ?? this.subjectId,
      createdAt: createdAt ?? this.createdAt,
      updatedAt: updatedAt ?? this.updatedAt,
    );
  }

  bool get isSuperAdmin => role == 'SUPER_ADMIN';
  bool get isTeacher => role == 'TEACHER';
  bool get isStudent => role == 'STUDENT';
  bool get isParent => role == 'PARENT';
}

class AuthResponse {
  final String token;
  final User user;

  AuthResponse({required this.token, required this.user});

  factory AuthResponse.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return AuthResponse(
      token: json['token']?.toString() ?? '',
      user: User.fromJson(json['user'] as Map<String, dynamic>? ?? {}),
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() {
    return {'token': token, 'user': user.toJson()};
  }
}

import 'dart:convert';
import 'quiz_model.dart';

class LessonModel {
  final String id;
  final String title;
  final String subject;
  final String description;
  final String videoUrl;
  final String? thumbnailUrl;
  final String? homeworkText;
  final QuizModel? quiz;
  final DateTime createdAt;
  final bool isPublic;
  final String teacherId;

  LessonModel({
    required this.id,
    required this.title,
    required this.subject,
    required this.description,
    required this.videoUrl,
    this.thumbnailUrl,
    this.homeworkText,
    this.quiz,
    required this.createdAt,
    this.isPublic = true,
    required this.teacherId,
  });

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() {
    return {
      'id': id,
      'title': title,
      'subject': subject,
      'description': description,
      'videoUrl': videoUrl,
      'thumbnailUrl': thumbnailUrl,
      'homeworkText': homeworkText,
      'quiz': quiz?.toJson(),
      'createdAt': createdAt.toIso8601String(),
      'isPublic': isPublic,
      'teacherId': teacherId,
    };
  }

  factory LessonModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return LessonModel(
      id: json['id'] as String,
      title: json['title'] as String,
      subject: json['subject'] as String,
      description: json['description'] as String,
      videoUrl: json['videoUrl'] as String,
      thumbnailUrl: json['thumbnailUrl'] as String?,
      homeworkText: json['homeworkText'] as String?,
      quiz: json['quiz'] != null
          ? QuizModel.fromJson(json['quiz'] as Map<String, dynamic>)
          : null,
      createdAt: DateTime.parse(json['createdAt'] as String),
      isPublic: json['isPublic'] as bool? ?? true,
      teacherId: json['teacherId'] as String,
    );
  }

  String toJsonString() => jsonEncode(toJson());

  factory LessonModel.fromJsonString(String jsonString) {
    return LessonModel.fromJson(jsonDecode(jsonString) as Map<String, dynamic>);
  }

  bool get hasQuiz => quiz != null && quiz!.questions.isNotEmpty;
  bool get hasHomework => homeworkText != null && homeworkText!.isNotEmpty;

  LessonModel copyWith({
    String? id,
    String? title,
    String? subject,
    String? description,
    String? videoUrl,
    String? thumbnailUrl,
    String? homeworkText,
    QuizModel? quiz,
    DateTime? createdAt,
    bool? isPublic,
    String? teacherId,
  }) {
    return LessonModel(
      id: id ?? this.id,
      title: title ?? this.title,
      subject: subject ?? this.subject,
      description: description ?? this.description,
      videoUrl: videoUrl ?? this.videoUrl,
      thumbnailUrl: thumbnailUrl ?? this.thumbnailUrl,
      homeworkText: homeworkText ?? this.homeworkText,
      quiz: quiz ?? this.quiz,
      createdAt: createdAt ?? this.createdAt,
      isPublic: isPublic ?? this.isPublic,
      teacherId: teacherId ?? this.teacherId,
    );
  }
}

import 'dart:convert';

enum QuizType { multipleChoice, essay }

class QuizQuestionModel {
  final String id;
  final String question;
  final QuizType type;
  final List<String> options;
  final int correctIndex;
  final String? essayAnswer;

  QuizQuestionModel({
    required this.id,
    required this.question,
    required this.type,
    required this.options,
    required this.correctIndex,
    this.essayAnswer,
  });

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() {
    return {
      'id': id,
      'question': question,
      'type': type.name,
      'options': options,
      'correctIndex': correctIndex,
      'essayAnswer': essayAnswer,
    };
  }

  factory QuizQuestionModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return QuizQuestionModel(
      id: json['id'] as String,
      question: json['question'] as String,
      type: QuizType.values.firstWhere(
        (e) => e.name == json['type'],
        orElse: () => QuizType.multipleChoice,
      ),
      options: List<String>.from(json['options'] as List),
      correctIndex: json['correctIndex'] as int,
      essayAnswer: json['essayAnswer'] as String?,
    );
  }
}

class QuizModel {
  final String id;
  final String title;
  final List<QuizQuestionModel> questions;
  final DateTime createdAt;
  final int timeLimitMinutes;
  final int passingScore;

  QuizModel({
    required this.id,
    required this.title,
    required this.questions,
    required this.createdAt,
    this.timeLimitMinutes = 30,
    this.passingScore = 60,
  });

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() {
    return {
      'id': id,
      'title': title,
      'questions': questions.map((q) => q.toJson()).toList(),
      'createdAt': createdAt.toIso8601String(),
      'timeLimitMinutes': timeLimitMinutes,
      'passingScore': passingScore,
    };
  }

  factory QuizModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return QuizModel(
      id: json['id'] as String,
      title: json['title'] as String,
      questions: (json['questions'] as List)
          .map((q) => QuizQuestionModel.fromJson(q as Map<String, dynamic>))
          .toList(),
      createdAt: DateTime.parse(json['createdAt'] as String),
      timeLimitMinutes: json['timeLimitMinutes'] as int? ?? 30,
      passingScore: json['passingScore'] as int? ?? 60,
    );
  }

  String toJsonString() => jsonEncode(toJson());

  factory QuizModel.fromJsonString(String jsonString) {
    return QuizModel.fromJson(jsonDecode(jsonString) as Map<String, dynamic>);
  }

  int get questionCount => questions.length;

  bool get isEmpty => questions.isEmpty;
}

class UserModel {

  final List<String> permissions;

  UserModel({
    required this.id,
    required this.name,
    required this.phoneNumber,
    required this.role,
    this.parentPhoneNumber,
    this.avatarUrl,
    this.isApproved = true,
    this.isVerified = false,
    this.subjectId,
    this.enrolledSubjectIds,
    this.permissions = const [],
  });

  factory UserModel.dummy() {
    return UserModel(
      id: '1',
      name: 'أحمد محمد',
      phoneNumber: '1234567890',
      role: 'student',
      parentPhoneNumber: '0987654321',
      avatarUrl: null,
      isApproved: true,
      isVerified: false,
      enrolledSubjectIds: ['math1', 'science1'],
      permissions: ['view_attendance', 'upload_videos'],
    );

      'isApproved': isApproved,
      'isVerified': isVerified,
      'subjectId': subjectId,
      'enrolledSubjectIds': enrolledSubjectIds,
      'permissions': permissions,
    };
  }

  factory UserModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return UserModel(
      id: json['id'] as String,
      name: json['name'] as String,
      phoneNumber: json['phoneNumber'] as String,
      role: json['role'] as String,
      parentPhoneNumber: json['parentPhoneNumber'] as String?,
      avatarUrl: json['avatarUrl'] as String?,
      isApproved: json['isApproved'] as bool? ?? true,
      isVerified: json['isVerified'] as bool? ?? false,
      subjectId: json['subjectId'] as String?,
      enrolledSubjectIds: (json['enrolledSubjectIds'] as List<dynamic>?)
          ?.map((e) => e as String)
          .toList(),
      permissions:
          (json['permissions'] as List<dynamic>?)
              ?.map((e) => e as String)
              .toList() ??
          [],
    );
  }

  UserModel copyWith({
    String? id,

    String? phoneNumber,
    String? role,
    String? parentPhoneNumber,
    String? avatarUrl,
    bool? isApproved,
    bool? isVerified,

    List<String>? enrolledSubjectIds,
    List<String>? permissions,

      permissions: permissions ?? this.permissions,
    );

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../core/constants/app_colors.dart';

class CustomBottomNav extends StatelessWidget {
  final int currentIndex;
  final Function(int) onTap;

  const CustomBottomNav({
    super.key,
    required this.currentIndex,
    required this.onTap,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final isDark = Theme.of(context).brightness == Brightness.dark;

    return Container(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: isDark ? AppColors.surfaceDark : AppColors.surfaceLight,
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
            blurRadius: 10,
            offset: const Offset(0, -5),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      child: BottomNavigationBar(
        currentIndex: currentIndex,
        onTap: onTap,
        type: BottomNavigationBarType.fixed,
        backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
        elevation: 0,
        selectedItemColor: AppColors.primaryAccent,
        unselectedItemColor: isDark
            ? AppColors.textSecondaryDark
            : AppColors.textSecondaryLight,
        items: const [
          BottomNavigationBarItem(
            icon: Icon(Icons.home_outlined),
            activeIcon: Icon(Icons.home),
            label: 'Home',
          ),
          BottomNavigationBarItem(
            icon: Icon(Icons.play_circle_outline),
            activeIcon: Icon(Icons.play_circle),
            label: 'Courses',
          ),
          BottomNavigationBarItem(
            icon: Icon(Icons.assignment_outlined),
            activeIcon: Icon(Icons.assignment),
            label: 'Tasks',
          ),
          BottomNavigationBarItem(
            icon: Icon(Icons.person_outline),
            activeIcon: Icon(Icons.person),
            label: 'Profile',
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../core/constants/app_colors.dart';

class CustomTextField extends StatelessWidget {
  final TextEditingController? controller;
  final String? hintText;
  final String? labelText;
  final IconData? prefixIcon;
  final Widget? suffixIcon;
  final bool obscureText;
  final TextInputType? keyboardType;
  final String? Function(String?)? validator;
  final void Function(String)? onChanged;
  final int maxLines;
  final bool enabled;

  const CustomTextField({
    super.key,
    this.controller,
    this.hintText,
    this.labelText,
    this.prefixIcon,
    this.suffixIcon,
    this.obscureText = false,
    this.keyboardType,
    this.validator,
    this.onChanged,
    this.maxLines = 1,
    this.enabled = true,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final isDark = Theme.of(context).brightness == Brightness.dark;
    final textColor = AppColors.getTextPrimaryColor(isDark);
    final hintColor = AppColors.getTextSecondaryColor(isDark);
    final surfaceColor = AppColors.getSurfaceColor(isDark);
    final borderColor = isDark ? Colors.transparent : const Color(0xFFE2E8F0);

    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        if (labelText != null) ...[
          Text(
            labelText!,
            style: TextStyle(color: textColor, fontWeight: FontWeight.w500),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
        ],
        TextFormField(
          controller: controller,
          obscureText: obscureText,
          keyboardType: keyboardType,
          validator: validator,
          onChanged: onChanged,
          maxLines: maxLines,
          enabled: enabled,
          style: TextStyle(color: textColor),
          decoration: InputDecoration(
            hintText: hintText,
            hintStyle: TextStyle(color: hintColor),
            prefixIcon: prefixIcon != null
                ? Icon(prefixIcon, color: hintColor)
                : null,
            suffixIcon: suffixIcon,
            filled: true,
            fillColor: surfaceColor,
            border: OutlineInputBorder(
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
              borderSide: BorderSide.none,
            ),
            enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
              borderSide: BorderSide(color: borderColor),
            ),
            focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
              borderSide: const BorderSide(
                color: AppColors.primaryAccent,
                width: 2,
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../core/constants/app_colors.dart';

class DynamicLogo extends StatelessWidget {
  final double size;
  final bool useIcon;
  final String? lightAssetPath;
  final String? darkAssetPath;

  const DynamicLogo({
    super.key,
    this.size = 80,
    this.useIcon = true,
    this.lightAssetPath,
    this.darkAssetPath,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final isDark = Theme.of(context).brightness == Brightness.dark;

    if (lightAssetPath != null && darkAssetPath != null) {
      return Image.asset(
        isDark ? darkAssetPath! : lightAssetPath!,
        width: size,
        height: size,
        errorBuilder: (context, error, stackTrace) => _buildIcon(isDark),
      );
    }

    if (!useIcon) {
      return ColorFiltered(
        colorFilter: ColorFilter.mode(
          isDark ? Colors.white : Colors.black,
          BlendMode.srcIn,
        ),
        child: Image.asset(
          'assets/logo.png',
          width: size,
          height: size,
          errorBuilder: (context, error, stackTrace) => _buildIcon(isDark),
        ),
      );
    }

    return _buildIcon(isDark);
  }

  Widget _buildIcon(bool isDark) {
    return Container(
      width: size,
      height: size,
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        gradient: AppColors.primaryGradient,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(size * 0.25),
      ),
      child: Icon(Icons.school, size: size * 0.5, color: Colors.white),
    );
  }
}

class DynamicIcon extends StatelessWidget {
  final IconData icon;
  final double? size;
  final Color? color;

  const DynamicIcon({super.key, required this.icon, this.size, this.color});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final isDark = Theme.of(context).brightness == Brightness.dark;
    final iconColor =
        color ??
        (isDark ? AppColors.textPrimaryDark : AppColors.textPrimaryLight);

    return Icon(icon, size: size, color: iconColor);
  }
}

class DynamicText extends StatelessWidget {
  final String text;
  final TextStyle? style;

  const DynamicText({super.key, required this.text, this.style});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final isDark = Theme.of(context).brightness == Brightness.dark;

    TextStyle defaultStyle;
    if (isDark) {
      defaultStyle = TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimaryDark);
    } else {
      defaultStyle = TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimaryLight);
    }

    return Text(text, style: defaultStyle.merge(style));
  }
}

class DynamicContainer extends StatelessWidget {
  final Widget child;
  final Color? lightColor;
  final Color? darkColor;
  final EdgeInsetsGeometry? padding;
  final EdgeInsetsGeometry? margin;
  final double? borderRadius;

  const DynamicContainer({
    super.key,
    required this.child,
    this.lightColor,
    this.darkColor,
    this.padding,
    this.margin,
    this.borderRadius,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final isDark = Theme.of(context).brightness == Brightness.dark;
    final backgroundColor = isDark
        ? (darkColor ?? AppColors.surfaceDark)
        : (lightColor ?? AppColors.surfaceLight);

    return Container(
      padding: padding,
      margin: margin,
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: backgroundColor,
        borderRadius: borderRadius != null
            ? BorderRadius.circular(borderRadius!)
            : null,
      ),
      child: child,
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../core/constants/app_colors.dart';

class GlassCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final Widget child;
  final EdgeInsetsGeometry? padding;
  final EdgeInsetsGeometry? margin;
  final double borderRadius;
  final bool useGradient;
  final VoidCallback? onTap;

  const GlassCard({
    super.key,
    required this.child,
    this.padding,
    this.margin,
    this.borderRadius = 16,
    this.useGradient = false,
    this.onTap,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final isDark = Theme.of(context).brightness == Brightness.dark;

    return Container(
      margin: margin,
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        gradient: useGradient ? AppColors.cardGradient : null,
        color: useGradient
            ? null
            : (isDark ? AppColors.surfaceDark : AppColors.surfaceLight),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(borderRadius),
        border: Border.all(
          color: isDark
              ? Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.1)
              : Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.05),
        ),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
            blurRadius: 10,
            offset: const Offset(0, 4),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      child: Material(
        color: Colors.transparent,
        child: InkWell(
          onTap: onTap,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(borderRadius),
          child: Padding(
            padding: padding ?? const EdgeInsets.all(16),
            child: child,
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class LoadingButton extends StatefulWidget {
  final VoidCallback? onPressed;
  final Widget child;
  final bool isLoading;
  final double? width;
  final EdgeInsetsGeometry? padding;

  const LoadingButton({
    super.key,
    required this.onPressed,
    required this.child,
    this.isLoading = false,
    this.width,
    this.padding,
  });

  @override
  State<LoadingButton> createState() => _LoadingButtonState();
}

class _LoadingButtonState extends State<LoadingButton> {
  bool _isLoading = false;

  @override
  void didUpdateWidget(LoadingButton oldWidget) {
    super.didUpdateWidget(oldWidget);
    if (widget.isLoading != oldWidget.isLoading) {
      setState(() {
        _isLoading = widget.isLoading;
      });
    }
  }

  Future<void> _handleTap() async {
    if (_isLoading || widget.onPressed == null) return;

    setState(() => _isLoading = true);

    widget.onPressed!();
    await Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 100));

    if (mounted) {
      setState(() => _isLoading = false);
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return SizedBox(
      width: widget.width,
      child: ElevatedButton(
        onPressed: (_isLoading || widget.onPressed == null) ? null : _handleTap,
        style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
          padding:
              widget.padding ??
              const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 24, vertical: 12),
        ),
        child: _isLoading
            ? const SizedBox(
                height: 20,
                width: 20,
                child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                  strokeWidth: 2,
                  valueColor: AlwaysStoppedAnimation<Color>(Colors.white),
                ),
              )
            : widget.child,
      ),
    );
  }
}

class LoadingOverlay extends StatelessWidget {
  final bool isLoading;
  final Widget child;
  final String? message;

  const LoadingOverlay({
    super.key,
    required this.isLoading,
    required this.child,
    this.message,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Stack(
      children: [
        child,
        if (isLoading)
          Container(
            color: Colors.black26,
            child: Center(
              child: Card(
                child: Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
                  child: Column(
                    mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                    children: [
                      const CircularProgressIndicator(),
                      if (message != null) ...[
                        const SizedBox(height: 16),
                        Text(message!),
                      ],
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';
import '../../core/providers/auth_provider.dart';

class PermissionWrapper extends StatelessWidget {
  final String permission;
  final Widget child;
  final Widget? fallback;
  final bool checkSuperAdmin;

  const PermissionWrapper({
    super.key,
    required this.permission,
    required this.child,
    this.fallback,
    this.checkSuperAdmin = true,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Consumer<AuthProvider>(
      builder: (context, auth, _) {
        final hasPermission = checkSuperAdmin && auth.isSuperAdmin
            ? true
            : auth.hasPermission(permission);

        if (!hasPermission && fallback != null) {
          return fallback!;
        }

        if (!hasPermission) {
          return const SizedBox.shrink();
        }

        return child;
      },
    );
  }
}

class PermissionBuilder extends StatelessWidget {
  final String permission;
  final Widget? builder;
  final Widget? fallback;

  const PermissionBuilder({
    super.key,
    required this.permission,
    this.builder,
    this.fallback,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Consumer<AuthProvider>(
      builder: (context, auth, _) {
        if (auth.isSuperAdmin || auth.hasPermission(permission)) {
          return builder ?? const SizedBox.shrink();
        }
        return fallback ?? const SizedBox.shrink();
      },
    );
  }
}

class PermissionButton extends StatelessWidget {
  final String permission;
  final VoidCallback onPressed;
  final Widget child;
  final Widget? fallback;

  const PermissionButton({
    super.key,
    required this.permission,
    required this.onPressed,
    required this.child,
    this.fallback,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Consumer<AuthProvider>(
      builder: (context, auth, _) {
        if (auth.isSuperAdmin || auth.hasPermission(permission)) {
          return GestureDetector(onTap: onPressed, child: child);
        }
        return fallback ?? const SizedBox.shrink();
      },
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../core/constants/app_colors.dart';

class PrimaryButton extends StatefulWidget {
  final String text;
  final VoidCallback? onPressed;
  final bool isLoading;
  final bool useGradient;
  final double? width;

  const PrimaryButton({
    super.key,
    required this.text,
    this.onPressed,
    this.isLoading = false,
    this.useGradient = true,
    this.width,
  });

  @override
  State<PrimaryButton> createState() => _PrimaryButtonState();
}

class _PrimaryButtonState extends State<PrimaryButton>
    with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  double _scale = 1.0;

  void _onTapDown(TapDownDetails details) {
    setState(() => _scale = 0.95);
  }

  void _onTapUp(TapUpDetails details) {
    setState(() => _scale = 1.0);
  }

  void _onTapCancel() {
    setState(() => _scale = 1.0);
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final isDark = Theme.of(context).brightness == Brightness.dark;
    final gradient = widget.useGradient
        ? AppColors.getPrimaryGradient(isDark)
        : null;

    return GestureDetector(
      onTapDown: widget.isLoading ? null : _onTapDown,
      onTapUp: widget.isLoading ? null : _onTapUp,
      onTapCancel: widget.isLoading ? null : _onTapCancel,
      child: AnimatedScale(
        scale: _scale,
        duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 100),
        child: SizedBox(
          width: widget.width ?? double.infinity,
          height: 56,
          child: DecoratedBox(
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              gradient: gradient,
              color: gradient == null ? AppColors.primaryAccent : null,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
              boxShadow: [
                BoxShadow(
                  color: AppColors.primaryAccent.withValues(
                    alpha: isDark ? 0.5 : 0.3,
                  ),
                  blurRadius: isDark ? 12 : 8,
                  offset: const Offset(0, 4),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            child: ElevatedButton(
              onPressed: widget.isLoading ? null : widget.onPressed,
              style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
                shadowColor: Colors.transparent,
                shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                ),
              ),
              child: widget.isLoading
                  ? const SizedBox(
                      width: 24,
                      height: 24,
                      child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                        strokeWidth: 2,
                        valueColor: AlwaysStoppedAnimation<Color>(Colors.white),
                      ),
                    )
                  : Text(
                      widget.text,
                      style: const TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 16,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                        color: Colors.white,
                      ),
                    ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';
import '../../core/providers/auth_provider.dart';
import '../../core/constants/user_role.dart';

class ProtectedRoute extends StatelessWidget {
  final Widget child;
  final List<UserRole> allowedRoles;
  final Widget? fallback;
  final bool requireVerification;

  const ProtectedRoute({
    super.key,
    required this.child,
    this.allowedRoles = const [],
    this.fallback,
    this.requireVerification = false,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Consumer<AuthProvider>(
      builder: (context, auth, _) {
        // Check if user is authenticated
        if (!auth.isAuthenticated) {
          return fallback ?? _buildRedirect(context);
        }

        // Check role authorization
        if (allowedRoles.isNotEmpty && auth.currentRole != null) {
          final hasAccess = allowedRoles.contains(auth.currentRole);

          if (!hasAccess) {
            return fallback ?? _buildAccessDenied(context);
          }
        }

        // Check verification requirement for teachers
        if (requireVerification && auth.isTeacher && !auth.isVerified) {
          return fallback ?? _buildVerificationRequired(context);
        }

        return child;
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _buildRedirect(BuildContext context) {
    return const _RedirectToLogin();
  }

  Widget _buildAccessDenied(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      body: Center(
        child: Column(
          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
          children: [
            const Icon(Icons.lock_outline, size: 64, color: Colors.red),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            const Text(
              'Access Denied',
              style: TextStyle(fontSize: 24, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            Text(
              'You do not have permission to access this page.',
              style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey[600]),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 24),
            ElevatedButton(
              onPressed: () {
                Navigator.of(context).pushReplacementNamed('/login');
              },
              child: const Text('Go to Login'),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildVerificationRequired(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      body: Center(
        child: Column(
          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
          children: [
            const Icon(Icons.pending_actions, size: 64, color: Colors.orange),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            const Text(
              'Account Pending Verification',
              style: TextStyle(fontSize: 24, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            Text(
              'Your teacher account is awaiting approval from the administrator.',
              style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey[600]),
              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 24),
            ElevatedButton(
              onPressed: () {
                Navigator.of(context).pushReplacementNamed('/login');
              },
              child: const Text('Go to Login'),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _RedirectToLogin extends StatelessWidget {
  const _RedirectToLogin();

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
      Navigator.of(context).pushReplacementNamed('/login');
    });
    return const Scaffold(body: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()));
  }
}

// Convenience widgets for common use cases
class AdminRoute extends StatelessWidget {
  final Widget child;
  final Widget? fallback;

  const AdminRoute({super.key, required this.child, this.fallback});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return ProtectedRoute(
      allowedRoles: const [UserRole.superAdmin],
      fallback: fallback,
      child: child,
    );
  }
}

  final Widget child;
  final Widget? fallback;
  final bool requireVerification;

  const TeacherRoute({
    super.key,
    required this.child,
    this.fallback,
    this.requireVerification = true,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return ProtectedRoute(
      allowedRoles: const [
        UserRole.superAdmin,
        UserRole.teacher,
        UserRole.supervisor,
        UserRole.assistant,
      ],
      fallback: fallback,
      requireVerification: requireVerification,
      child: child,
    );
  }
}

  @override

      child: child,
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';
import '../../../core/constants/app_colors.dart';
import '../../../core/providers/theme_provider.dart';

class ThemeToggle extends StatelessWidget {
  const ThemeToggle({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Consumer<ThemeProvider>(
      builder: (context, themeProvider, child) {
        return IconButton(
          onPressed: () => themeProvider.toggleTheme(),
          icon: AnimatedSwitcher(
            duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
            transitionBuilder: (child, animation) {
              return RotationTransition(
                turns: animation,
                child: FadeTransition(opacity: animation, child: child),
              );
            },
            child: Icon(
              themeProvider.isDarkMode ? Icons.light_mode : Icons.dark_mode,
              key: ValueKey(themeProvider.isDarkMode),
              color: themeProvider.isDarkMode
                  ? Colors.amber
                  : AppColors.textSecondaryLight,
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class UploadProgressDialog extends StatefulWidget {
  final String fileName;
  final int sent;
  final int total;
  final VoidCallback? onCancel;

  const UploadProgressDialog({
    super.key,
    required this.fileName,
    required this.sent,
    required this.total,
    this.onCancel,
  });

  @override
  State<UploadProgressDialog> createState() => _UploadProgressDialogState();
}

class _UploadProgressDialogState extends State<UploadProgressDialog> {
  double get progress => widget.total > 0 ? widget.sent / widget.total : 0;
  int get percentage => (progress * 100).round();

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Dialog(
      shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20)),
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            const Icon(Icons.cloud_upload, size: 48, color: Color(0xFF0B101B)),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            Text(
              'Uploading ${widget.fileName}',
              style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
              maxLines: 2,
              overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            LinearProgressIndicator(
              value: progress,
              backgroundColor: Colors.grey[300],
              valueColor: const AlwaysStoppedAnimation<Color>(
                Color(0xFF0B101B),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            Text(
              '$percentage%',
              style: TextStyle(
                fontSize: 24,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                color: Colors.grey[700],
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            Text(
              _getProgressMessage(percentage),
              style: TextStyle(fontSize: 14, color: Colors.grey[600]),
            ),
            if (widget.onCancel != null) ...[
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              TextButton(
                onPressed: widget.onCancel,
                child: const Text(
                  'Cancel',
                  style: TextStyle(color: Colors.red),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  String _getProgressMessage(int percentage) {
    if (percentage < 25) {
      return 'Preparing upload...';
    } else if (percentage < 50) {
      return 'Uploading video...';
    } else if (percentage < 75) {
      return 'Uploading thumbnail...';
    } else if (percentage < 100) {
      return 'Finalizing...';
    } else {
      return 'Complete!';
    }
  }
}

class StatusDialog extends StatelessWidget {

              title,

              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            Text(
              message,
              style: TextStyle(fontSize: 14, color: Colors.grey[600]),
              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 24),
            SizedBox(
              width: double.infinity,
              child: ElevatedButton(
                onPressed: () {
                  if (onConfirm != null) {
                    onConfirm!();

                },

                  foregroundColor: Colors.white,
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 12),
                  shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                  ),
                ),
                child: Text(confirmText),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
